## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Rework the identity model: Local Players vs Accounts

## Context

Multiplayer works, but the account/profile onboarding is backwards and incomplete, and the underlying identity model doesn't match how we want the game to feel:

- **Login is blocked behind profile creation.** On first run (`components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx:27`), the zero-profiles branch renders `CreateProfileForm` full-screen and *hides* `<AccountPanel/>` (the Sign in / Register / online controls, only rendered in the profile-list view at `ProfileSelect.tsx:44`). A brand-new player literally cannot reach login/register until a local profile exists.
- **Guest mode is a one-way street.** In `components/menu/AccountPanel.tsx:94-97` a guest (`user.isAnonymous`) is offered *only* "Keep my worlds — create account". `signOut()` exists solely in the non-anonymous branch (`:103`), so a guest can never get back to the login screen.

Beyond the two bugs, we're deliberately **redefining the identity model** (this reverses the current "guests first" design in `docs/online.md`):

- **Local Player** (logged out): keeps many **local profiles** in the browser (localStorage, exactly as today), owns local single-player worlds, makes **zero** server calls. Cannot create, invite to, *or join* anything online. Entry point is **"Play Locally"**; the list is **"Local Profiles"**.
- **Account** (logged in): owns up to **5 online profiles** stored server-side that sync across devices; each profile owns up to **10 online worlds**; can host, invite, and join. When logged in you see **only** your account's online profiles and their worlds — existing local worlds are **hidden but preserved** (never deleted, never auto-uploaded), and reappear when you play as a Local Player again.

Guests-as-anonymous-server-users go away entirely: online == you have an account.

**Sequencing:** ship as three landable phases. Phase 1 fixes the two reported bugs on its own and is low-risk. Phases 2–3 build the new model and retire the guest layer.

---

## Target model (agreed rules)

| | Local Player (logged out) | Account (logged in) |
|---|---|---|
| Profiles | many, local (localStorage) | up to **5**, server-side, synced |
| Worlds | local single-player only | online only, up to **10 per profile** |
| Online create / invite / join | ❌ none | ✅ |
| Storage | browser only, no network | server (`worlds` rows + `saveBlob`) |
| On login | local worlds **hidden but preserved** | shows only account's online profiles/worlds |

---

## Phase 1 — Entry-UX fix (the two reported bugs) · independently shippable

Small, low-risk, no data-model change. Keeps today's backend semantics.

1. **Login reachable on first run** — `components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx`. Render `<AccountPanel/>` in the first-run (`firstRun`) branch too, above the create-profile form, so Sign in / Register are always reachable. Safe for offline-first: `AccountPanel`'s effect only calls the server when `onlineUsed()` is true (`AccountPanel.tsx:24`), so a brand-new offline player still makes no auth request.
2. **Guest can return to login** — `components/menu/AccountPanel.tsx:94-97`. In the `user.isAnonymous` branch add a **"Sign out"** button mirroring the existing non-anonymous handler (`:102-105`: `await authClient().signOut(); await refresh();`), which drops back to the "Offline" state where "Sign in" / "Play online as guest" already live.
3. **Tests** — update `components/menu/ProfileSelect.test.tsx` (the "no profiles → no cancel" test now also expects the account panel present) and `components/menu/AccountPanel.test.tsx` (add: a guest can sign out and returns to "Offline").
4. **Docs/changelog** — `CHANGELOG.md` entry; note the fix in `docs/online.md`.

---

## Phase 2 — Local vs Account identity, server profiles, quotas

The core feature. Introduces a server-side profile entity and makes the menu auth-aware.

### Server: profiles table + per-profile worlds
- **Schema** — add a `profiles` table to `db/schema.ts` and `db/ddl.ts` (mirror `user`/`worlds` style): `{ id, ownerId → user.id (cascade), name, skinId, createdAt }`. Add a nullable `profileId → profiles.id` column to the `worlds` table (the account still owns via `ownerId`; `profileId` says *which* profile within the account). Add a drizzle migration under `db/migrations`, and a data backfill: for each existing account create one default profile (from the account's `name` + `skinId`) and stamp its existing worlds with that `profileId`.
- **Service + quotas** — in `lib/online/worldsService.ts`: add profile CRUD (`listProfiles`/`createProfile`/`renameProfile`/`setProfileSkin`/`deleteProfile`, scoped to `ownerId`) and enforce quotas at write time — reject the 6th profile per account and the 11th world per profile (count-before-insert, returning the existing `failure`/`STATUS` shape). Put the limits in `config.ts` (`MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES = 5`, `MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE = 10`) so they're documented in `docs/tuning.md`.
- **Routes** — add `app/api/profiles/route.ts` (GET list / POST create) and `app/api/profiles/[id]/route.ts` (PATCH / DELETE), thin adapters over the service using the existing `sessionUser` + `unauthorized()` pattern from `lib/online/http.ts`.
- **Gate online to accounts** — surface `isAnonymous` in `sessionUser` (`lib/online/http.ts` currently drops it) and reject anonymous users in `createWorld`, `createInvite`, and `mintTicket` (`worldsService.ts`). One choke point; routes need no change.
- **Ticket identity = the profile** — `mintTicket` should stamp `name`/`skinId` from the *selected profile*, not the account. The ticket route (`app/api/worlds/[id]/ticket/route.ts`) takes a `profileId`, validated to belong to the caller. (Decide during impl whether the game server's per-player save slice keys on account `sub` or on `profileId` — see Open decisions.)

### Client
- **Online profiles wrapper** — new `lib/online/profilesClient.ts` mirroring `lib/online/onlineClient.ts` (total functions returning `null`/`false`): `listOnlineProfiles`, `createOnlineProfile`, `renameOnlineProfile`, `deleteOnlineProfile`.
- **Auth-aware ProfileSelect** — `components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx` becomes the hub. Logged out → local profiles from `lib/game/profiles.ts` under a **"Play Locally"** heading (unchanged behavior). Logged in → fetch and list **online profiles**; local worlds are simply not shown. The account panel gains a clear **Sign out** → returns to local mode. Rename UI copy: guest/"Play online as guest" → "Play Locally" / "Sign in / Register".
- **World lists** — `components/menu/WorldSelect.tsx` already splits local / Cloud Saves / Online Worlds. In account mode it shows the selected online profile's worlds (server `worlds` filtered by `profileId`), reusing `createOnlineWorld`/`listOnlineWorlds`/invite helpers. The existing `sp-cloud` cloud-save storage layer (`lib/game/cloudSaves.ts`, `putSaveBlob`/`getSaveBlob`) is the storage for account worlds — no new blob path needed.
- **GameShell wiring** — `components/GameShell.tsx` threads the selected profile (local `Profile` or online profile) into play, and passes `profileId` to `playOnline` → ticket request.

### Tests
- `lib/online/worldsService.test.ts` (or existing service tests): quota enforcement (6th profile, 11th world), anonymous rejection on create/invite/ticket, ticket carries profile identity.
- Component tests for the auth-aware `ProfileSelect` (local vs account mode) and the new profiles client.
- Update the coverage map in `docs/testing.md`.

---

## Phase 3 — Retire the anonymous-guest layer, rewire invites, migrate, docs

1. **Remove anonymous auth** — delete the `anonymous()` plugin + `onLinkAccount` re-parenting (`lib/auth/server.ts:41-68`), `anonymousClient()` and the `ensureSignedIn` anonymous path (`lib/auth/client.ts:22,58-65`), the `isAnonymous` column (`db/schema.ts:32`, `db/ddl.ts`), and the guest branches in `AccountPanel.tsx`. The game server and ticket format need no change (they never referenced anonymity).
2. **Invites require sign-in** — `app/join/[token]/page.tsx:26` currently calls `ensureSignedIn()` (mints a guest). Change unauthenticated visitors to a real Sign in / Register step (the world name still previews via the no-auth `GET /api/invite/:token`), then accept the invite, then **pick which profile** joins.
3. **Migration of existing deployed data** — accounts and their worlds are safe (backfilled in Phase 2). Existing **guest-owned** online worlds/memberships can't survive removing anonymous users; default plan: drop them via the migration (or leave orphaned) and call it out in release notes. Existing **local** profiles/worlds are untouched.
4. **Docs** — rewrite `docs/online.md` "Identity: guests first" → "accounts for online, Local Players for offline"; update `docs/save-format.md` (server profiles, per-profile worlds), `docs/protocol.md` (ticket carries profile identity), `docs/reference.md`/`docs/manual.md` (quotas, Play Locally), and `CHANGELOG.md`. Remove/replace `lib/auth/auth.test.ts` (the onLinkAccount test) and the guest-path e2e in `e2e/multiplayer.e2e.ts`.

---

## Branching, commits & PRs

- **Phase 1** (reported bugs) ships **first, on its own** — a small focused PR (or straight to `main`, since it's a pure bug fix with no model change), so live users get the fix without waiting on the redesign.
- **Phases 2 + 3** land **together** on one branch `feat/local-vs-account-identity` off `main`, as **one PR** — they must ship as a unit so production never sees the half-migrated state (anonymous guests still minting while account-profiles are already live). Within the PR, one focused Conventional-Commit per slice: schema+migration → service+quotas → profile routes → auth-aware client/UI → remove anonymous layer → invite rewire → docs/tests. Keep `bun run lint` green at each commit (bisectable).
- Why-focused commit bodies; **no** `Co-Authored-By` and **no** AI-attribution lines in the PR description. Open against `hutusi/monecraft` `main`. Pushing / opening PRs is user-authorized — not done until you ask.

## Verification

- **Per phase:** `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build` (the CI `verify` gate); `bun run test:e2e` for Phases touching the React shell / renderer (all three).
- **Phase 1 manual:** fresh browser (clear localStorage) → first screen shows both Sign in / Register **and** the create-profile form (login no longer blocked). Enter guest → a "Sign out" returns to the login screen.
- **Phase 2 manual (zero-service online):** run with `DATABASE_URL=pglite://memory` + `PERSISTENCE=memory` (`docs/online.md` "No Docker at all"): sign in → see online profiles (not local worlds); create a 6th profile → rejected; create an 11th world under a profile → rejected; local worlds reappear after Sign out.
- **Phase 3 manual:** an invite link while logged out routes to Sign in / Register (no silent guest); after signing in and picking a profile, the world joins. Confirm no `signIn.anonymous` path remains (grep).

## Open decisions / risks (resolve during implementation)

- **Save-slice keying for shared MP worlds:** account (`sub`) vs `profileId`. Simplest MVP keys by account; profile-level identity is the "purer" model but touches save v-format + the game server. Decide in Phase 2.
- **Existing guest-owned online worlds** on the live deployment: confirm "drop with release-note warning" is acceptable, or add a one-time upgrade prompt before Phase 3 ships.
- **Offline-first invariant** stays intact: no server call for a logged-out Local Player (keep the `onlineUsed()` gate before any session fetch).

```

# Changelog

All notable changes to this project are documented in this file.

## [Unreleased]

### Added

- **Cloud saves — single-player worlds across devices**: signed in, you can now make a single-player world follow you between browsers and machines. An **Upload to cloud** action on a world's card copies it to your account (the card then reads **☁ Synced**) and syncs every autosave up from then on; on another device your synced worlds appear under a **Cloud Saves** section to **Download** and play. It's last-write-wins with a safe open-time reconcile: opening a synced world adopts the newer cloud copy only when it actually advanced past this device — so a world you played offline keeps its newer progress instead of being clobbered — and a losing write during play warns you and stops syncing rather than overwriting the other device. Opt-in per world; worlds you never upload stay fully offline, and this is a local-manifest link (`WorldMeta.cloudId`) — the world save format is unchanged. Wires up the cloud-save transport that shipped as groundwork in v0.13.0.
- **Online co-op — in-game player list & owner kick**: every online world now shows a **player list** in the top-right corner, and the world **owner** gets a **Kick** button beside each other player to eject a griefer without leaving the world (free the cursor with `Esc` to click it — the panel sits above the pause overlay). It rides a new owner-only `kick` protocol message that the game server re-checks against the sender's signed ticket role (a member's forged kick is dropped, and you can't kick yourself), reusing the same in-process eject as the admin endpoint — no web→game admin bridge, so the game server stays stateless. The `welcome` frame now carries each player's own role.
- **Online co-op parity — per-player progression, kill credit & pets**: each player in a shared world now earns their **own** advancements and statistics (the engine attributes an emitted gameplay event to whoever's per-player step or command is running, so a co-op room scores everyone independently instead of accruing nothing) and gets credit for their **own** kills — melee, arrow, spear, or a companion pet's bite — tracked on the mob so even a delayed death (a daylight burn, an explosion softened by your earlier hit) credits the right player, paying them the loot and XP. Your advancement set and stats sync privately over the `SelfDelta`, advancement toasts are tagged so you only see your own, and **pets follow their owner** rather than trailing the nearest player. Single-player is unchanged.
- **Online co-op parity — vehicles & arrows (protocol v1→v2)**: rafts and ships are now boardable in a shared world, and in-flight arrows are replicated (not just their hits). Vehicles and arrows travel on their own per-tick channels (`vp`/`prj`, mirroring the mob-pose skeleton) and the join world-sync, so the existing renderer draws them unchanged. A **mounted** rider is the one hybrid-model exception: the server owns their position (driven from their input intents) and streams it on the private `SelfDelta` (`mountedVehicleId` + authoritative position), so the client replica stops predicting its own motion while mounted rather than rubber-banding against the boat — and the server no longer answers a mounted player's (expected-rejected) pose with a `forcePose` snap-back. The version bump means a stale v1 client is cleanly refused at the door during a rollover. Single-player is unchanged; the boarding gate that made vehicles single-player-only is lifted.

## [0.13.0] - 2026-07-04

### Added

- **Multiplayer hardening & ops**: the online session now survives dropped connections and ships an operator toolkit. The replica engine outlives its socket — a non-fatal drop (idle timeout, redeploy, network blip) walks a back-off ladder (1/2/4/8/8 s), mints a **fresh** join ticket each rung, and re-handshakes to re-sync the same world in place (an amber "Reconnecting…" badge, not a dead-end modal); fatal closes (bad ticket, kick, slow-client) don't retry. A `setSimulatedLatency(ms)` knob on `window.__monecraft.net` (and `NEXT_PUBLIC_NET_SIM_LATENCY_MS`) injects artificial delay for local testing. World owners can **revoke every outstanding invite link** in one click from the menu. The game server gains admin ops behind `ADMIN_TOKEN`: a per-room replay log (`GET /rooms/:id/log`) that `scripts/replay.ts` reconstructs offline, an owner **kick** (`POST /rooms/:id/kick/:playerId`), and `/rooms` bandwidth (`kbOutPerSec`) alongside the tick gauges — plus `scripts/loadSim.ts`, a synthetic N-client soak that reports server tick p95 vs the 50 ms budget for tuning `MAX_ROOMS`. Net constants are documented in tuning.md and an ops runbook in online.md.
- **Online co-op multiplayer**: 2–8 players in one shared, server-hosted world via a private invite link — the offline single-player game is entirely unchanged and still needs no account or connection. The menu grows an **Online Worlds** section (create a server-backed world, copy an invite link) and an **Account** panel (one-click guest, or upgrade to keep your worlds); opening an invite link (`/join/<token>`) signs the visitor in — as a guest if they're new — accepts the membership, and drops the world into their list. In-world you see the other players (named avatars overhead), share every block edit / mob / chest / day-night with them, and **chat** with `T`/`Enter`; a ping badge and disconnect notice track the connection. The client mounts the **same** renderer/audio/HUD/input stack on a _replica_ engine (`NetworkSession`, `lib/net/`): it worldgens byte-identically from the server's seed, applies the gzipped world sync, predicts only its own movement (streamed at tick rate; the server clamps speed/teleports and owns hunger/health/inventory via change-only self-deltas), renders remote players and mobs ~125 ms in the past from interpolation buffers, and reroutes gameplay commands up the wire through the engine's `routeDispatch` hook — so the input controller and every UI panel behave exactly as in single-player. Pause is personal (the world keeps running; "Save & Quit" becomes "Leave world"), the night skips only when everyone's in bed, and mining is server-confirmed (~1 RTT). A full-stack Playwright spec (`e2e/multiplayer.e2e.ts`) drives two browsers through invite → join → shared edit → chat against the real Next app (on an in-process `pglite://memory` Postgres) and Bun game server, no external services. **v1 scope cuts**: rafts/ships aren't boardable online, in-flight arrows aren't replicated (only their hits), and advancements/stats accrue to the room's primary player only.
- **Multiplayer game server (groundwork)**: a Bun process hosting authoritative world rooms over WebSocket — protocol v1 (JSON envelopes + one gzipped world-sync frame; total validation of hostile input with a per-command allow-list), 20 Hz fixed-tick rooms with client-pose clamping (sprint-ceiling speed, jump/fall vertical, teleport rejection → forcePose) that keeps hunger/stats/fall-damage server-authoritative, change-only per-player self-deltas, deadbanded 10 Hz mob replication with 5 s keyframes, chat with rate limits, backpressure shedding + slow-client kicks, join-restore/leave-persist through the v17 save, 60 s dirty persistence + SIGTERM drain, idle room eviction, /health + token-guarded /rooms diagnostics, PERSISTENCE=memory for DB-less runs, a netProbe CLI client, and Dockerfile + fly.toml. The engine gains authority-aware remote-pose application and can construct playerless rooms (bootPlayer: false).
- **Online accounts, cloud saves & invites (groundwork)**: better-auth identities with instant anonymous guests — upgrading to a real account (sign-up or sign-in while playing as guest) re-parents the guest's worlds/memberships/invites onto the new account, proven by an integration test against real better-auth + real SQL (PGlite). Drizzle/Postgres schema for worlds, memberships, and invite links; membership-gated world CRUD; idempotent invite acceptance with expiry/max-uses; gzipped single-player cloud-save blobs with last-write-wins conflict detection; and 60-second HS256 join tickets — the only trust link the future game server needs (it never sees cookies). Menu gains an Account panel. Fully offline-first: until the player takes an online action, the client makes no auth requests at all.

### Changed

- **Multi-player engine core** (save **v16→v17**; single-player behavior unchanged — the full e2e suite passes as before): the engine now models N players in one world, with single-player as the one-entry case of the same state. `GameState.players: Map<PlayerId, PlayerState>` absorbs every per-player field (inventory, vitals, effects/xp/stats/advancements, death/respawn, mining/fishing/vehicle, per-player timers); the old flat fields remain as accessor aliases onto the primary player for the shell/tests/console, with an ESLint guard banning them inside engine systems. All 23 systems take the acting player explicitly: mobs hunt the nearest targetable player (Creative/Spectator excluded) and anchor flee/pet-follow on the nearest of any mode; arrows/explosions hit any/all players in range; spawn directors center waves on a random party member and scale the hostile cap by `ceil(players/2)`; dungeon spawners arm for any player in radius; random ticks split their budget across players; raid rewards pay every living player. The night skips only when **every** eligible player is in bed (new `wakeUp` command backs out pre-fade). `dispatch`/`applyLook` take the commanding player's id; `addPlayer`/`removePlayer` (join restores the persisted v17 slice, leave serializes it back), `setPlayerInput`, and the `authority`/`headless` engine options are the surface a multiplayer server drives. Saves migrate automatically (v16 flat fields wrap into `players: [{id:"local",…}]`; pet owners become real player ids).
- **Input intents** (no behavior change): the engine's `FrameInput` no longer carries DOM key codes, CapsLock, or pointer-lock state — the input controller reduces them to abstract movement intents (`forward/back/left/right/jump/sprint/crouch` + `mineHeld`). Key bindings and cursor capture now live entirely in `lib/game/input/inputController.ts`; the engine simulates the same regardless of input source, which is what a multiplayer client's per-tick intent packets (and a future keybinding screen) require.
- **Tick driver extraction** (no behavior change): the rAF loop's catch-up substepping moved from `useMinecraftGame` into `lib/game/engine/tickDriver.ts` (`createAccumulator`), alongside a new drift-corrected `createFixedTicker` — the 20 Hz driver a headless multiplayer server will step worlds with. Unit-tested with a fake clock (catch-up, debt-drop cap, no drift under chronically-late callbacks).
- **Portable worldgen noise** (`WORLDGEN_VERSION` **10→11**, save schema stays v16): all seed-determined noise — terrain waves, biome fields, cave-walk trig, underground speckle/flora hashes, weather windows, the daylight curve — moved from engine-defined `Math.sin` to bit-portable implementations (`lib/world/noise.ts`), so the same seed now generates **byte-identical worlds on every JS engine**. This is the groundwork for online multiplayer (a Bun server and browser clients must agree on the generated baseline to sync worlds as seed + block diff) and retires the fragile "worldgen hashes are only valid on the pinned Bun" caveat. Terrain is visually unchanged (same formulas; every structural probe passed the re-baseline untouched), but exact bytes shift: **existing worlds reboot from their seed and discard block edits**, per the standard worldgen-bump policy. Digests re-baselined into the new `lib/world/generationBaselines.ts`; a new Playwright spec (`e2e/determinism.e2e.ts`) proves Chromium/V8 regenerates the exact Bun/JSC baseline.

## [0.12.0] - 2026-07-03

### Added

- **The aquatic update**: the ocean the new rafts and ships sail is now worth sailing — fish, flora, wrecks to dive, and treasure to hunt, all on **zero binary assets**. Bumps **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` 9→10** (kelp/coral/wrecks/buried chests write generated blocks, so existing worlds reboot from their seed and every worldgen/world-type/meshing digest except flat was re-baselined per docs/testing.md) with **no save-schema change** — the schema stays **v16**.
  - **Kelp & coral**: the sandy ocean floor grows swaying **kelp stalks** and pink/blue **reef coral** (a new hash-gated `placeOceanFlora` pass that consumes no shared PRNG). Kelp is a solid cube painted to read as a plant (the wheat trick — no new geometry): stalks always keep a **3-block clear-water band** below the surface so boats and fishing casts never snag, growth via the existing random-tick sampler enforces the same cap, and **breaking a cell breaks the stalk above it, refilling submerged cells with water — never air**. Kelp smelts **3-at-a-time into dried kelp** (a 2-hunger snack); corals are decorative salvage. New `BlockId`s **appended** (55–57)
  - **Cod & salmon**: the first water-dwelling mobs. A dedicated **aquatic AI branch** (3D wander/flee, every move gated on the destination cell being water, no ground clamp) runs before any land logic; a beached fish flops and suffocates. Both drop **raw fish** — the recipe-book hint for it now reads "Hunt a cod". Fish are fungible (never persisted; old saves load cleanly), spawn as schools in whatever water lies near spawn, and a new **aquatic spawn director** trickles them in around the player (up to `AQUATIC_CAP` 24) so far-flung oceans are stocked too — failing closed on dry worlds (Superflat spawns none). Legless "fish" model variant on `createMobModel`, bubble-blip ZZFX calls
  - **Shipwrecks**: flooded plank hulls (broken walls, snapped mast, always fully submerged) sink onto the deep ocean floor on their own decoupled PRNG, holding 1–2 chests of nautical salvage — and the **treasure map** at a 40% roll. The dungeon lazy-fill generalizes to `fillWorldgenChestIfUnlooted`: the site set holding a chest's index picks both the loot table and a family-distinct seed constant, and **all families share the persisted `lootedChests` field unchanged** (no re-roll exploit, no save bump)
  - **Buried treasure & the treasure map**: hold a map (wreck loot, or a rare fishing catch) and a **compass pill** points at the _nearest_ unearthed hoard while red **X**s mark the minimap; dig two blocks under the beach sand for the richest chest table in the game (emeralds guaranteed). The map is deliberately **stateless** — no per-item target, no save field: the compass derives everything from the seed and retargets automatically after each dig. Chests place on a fourth decoupled PRNG with a **seed-pure beach proxy** (terrain-height band + a below-sea neighbor column — never post-water block reads), sand-capped so a breaching cave can't expose them. First dig fires the new **treasureUnearthed** event → **X Marks the Spot** advancement + a **Treasures Unearthed** statistic
- **Rafts and ships**: craftable water vehicles on **zero binary assets**. Hold a **raft** (4 planks + 2 wood) or **ship** (8 planks + 3 wood + 2 string), right-click a water surface to place it, then travel without swimming. Rafts are smaller and slower and require right-click to board; ships are larger/faster and auto-board when you step onto the deck. Movement uses the existing WASD controls while mounted, and crouch dismounts to a nearby safe spot. Placed vehicles persist across reloads, so the save schema bumps **v15 → v16** with an additive `vehicles` field; vehicle items are string ids and vehicles are entities, not blocks, so **no worldgen change** (`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 9). Rafts and ships live in their own **Vehicles** crafting category; placing plays a wooden thunk and a failed placement (no open water, blocked, or at the **`MAX_VEHICLES`** (64) per-world cap) plays a "can't place here" denial cue. A saved boat is never dropped on reload even if the water beneath it was later removed, and boat travel no longer inflates the walked-distance statistic.
- **Generated villages, villager professions & raids** (Living World, PR 2 of 2): the world is now inhabited and contested.
```

1	import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
2	import { acquirePointerLock } from "./helpers";
3	
4	/**
5	 * The full co-op journey against the real online stack: the Next app backed
6	 * by an in-process Postgres (DATABASE_URL=pglite://memory) plus the Bun game
7	 * server, both from playwright.config's webServer list — no Docker, no cloud.
8	 * Two isolated browser contexts play two guests: the host creates an online
9	 * world through the menus, the friend joins through the invite link, and the
10	 * pair must see each other, share block edits, and chat.
11	 */
12	
13	/** Console/page errors collected like the smoke fixture does (favicon 404 is noise). */
14	function watchErrors(page: Page, sink: string[]): void {
15	  page.on("console", (message) => {
16	    if (message.type() !== "error") return;
17	    if (message.text().includes("Failed to load resource") && message.location().url.endsWith("/favicon.ico")) return;
18	    sink.push(`${message.text()} (${message.location().url})`);
19	  });
20	  page.on("pageerror", (error) => sink.push(String(error)));
21	}
22	
23	/** Booted, synced, and drawing: the bar every online entry must clear. */
24	async function waitForOnlineGame(page: Page): Promise<void> {
25	  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__monecraft !== undefined, undefined, { timeout: 30000 });
26	  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__monecraft!.net?.status() === "online", undefined, { timeout: 30000 });
27	  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__monecraft!.renderer.renderedTriangles() > 0, undefined, { timeout: 30000 });
28	}
29	
30	test("two guests share an online world via an invite link", async ({ browser }) => {
31	  // Two production builds of the game plus a WebSocket handshake each; CI
32	  // renders with software GL, so the whole journey gets a generous ceiling.
33	  test.setTimeout(240000);
34	  const errors: string[] = [];
35	
36	  // ── the host: guest account → online world → in-game ─────────────────────
37	  const hostContext = await browser.newContext();
38	  const host = await hostContext.newPage();
39	  watchErrors(host, errors);
40	  // Seed a profile so the menu opens on the profile LIST (where the account
41	  // panel lives) instead of the first-run create form.
42	  await host.addInitScript(() => {
43	    if (!localStorage.getItem("minecraft_profiles_v1")) {
44	      localStorage.setItem(
45	        "minecraft_profiles_v1",
46	        JSON.stringify({ version: 1, profiles: [{ id: "host-profile", name: "Hosta", skinId: "default", createdAt: 1 }], activeProfileId: "host-profile" })
47	      );
48	    }
49	  });
50	  await host.goto("/");
51	
52	  await host.getByRole("button", { name: "Play online as guest" }).click();
53	  await expect(host.getByText("Playing as guest")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
54	  await host.getByTestId("profile-host-profile").click();
55	
56	  // Creative, so the host's block breaks are instant on the server side.
57	  await host.getByTestId("new-online-world").click();
58	  await host.getByLabel("World name").fill("Co-op Test");
59	  await host.getByLabel("World seed").fill("4242");
60	  await host.getByRole("button", { name: "Creative mode" }).click();
61	  await host.getByRole("button", { name: "Create World" }).click();
62	  await waitForOnlineGame(host);
63	
64	  // Mint the invite with the host's cookies (the menu's Copy button wraps the
65	  // same endpoint; the clipboard is out of reach in headless runs).
66	  const worldId = await host.evaluate(async () => {
67	    const response = await fetch("/api/worlds");
68	    const { worlds } = (await response.json()) as { worlds: Array<{ id: string }> };
69	    return worlds[0].id;
70	  });
71	  const inviteToken = await host.evaluate(async (id) => {
72	    const response = await fetch(`/api/worlds/${id}/invites`, { method: "POST" });
73	    const { token } = (await response.json()) as { token: string };
74	    return token;
75	  }, worldId);
76	
77	  // ── the friend: invite link → guest account → same world ─────────────────
78	  const friendContext = await browser.newContext();
79	  const friend = await friendContext.newPage();
80	  watchErrors(friend, errors);
81	  await friend.goto(`/join/${inviteToken}`);
82	  await expect(friend.getByText(/You've joined/)).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
83	  await friend.getByRole("link", { name: "Open the game" }).click();
84	
85	  // First run in this fresh context: create the friend's local profile; the
86	  // invited world is already waiting in the Online Worlds section.
87	  await friend.getByLabel("Profile name").fill("Friend");
88	  await friend.getByRole("button", { name: "Create" }).click();
89	  await friend.getByTestId(`online-world-${worldId}`).click();
90	  await waitForOnlineGame(friend);
91	
92	  // ── both replicas agree there are two players in the room ────────────────
93	  for (const page of [host, friend]) {
94	    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.players.size === 2, undefined, { timeout: 15000 });
95	  }
96	
97	  // ── a block edit crosses the wire ─────────────────────────────────────────
98	  // The host digs straight down. The replica's own mining is cosmetic (it
99	  // never completes a break locally), so a journal entry appearing on BOTH
100	  // clients proves the server decided the break and broadcast it.
101	  await acquirePointerLock(host);
102	  await host.waitForTimeout(1000); // settle (slow CI renderers need the margin)
103	  await host.evaluate(() => {
104	    window.__monecraft!.engine.state.player.pitch = -Math.PI / 2 + 0.02;
105	  });
106	  await host.mouse.down();
107	  for (const page of [host, friend]) {
108	    await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.blockChanges.changes().length), { timeout: 30000 }).toBeGreaterThan(0);
109	  }
110	  await host.mouse.up();
111	
112	  // ── chat round-trips, rendering in the other player's log ────────────────
113	  await host.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.net!.sendChat("hello from the host"));
114	  await expect(friend.getByText("hello from the host")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 });
115	  await friend.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.net!.sendChat("hi back"));
116	  await expect(host.getByText("hi back")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 });
117	
118	  // ── the roster lists both players; only the owner (host) gets a Kick control ─
119	  for (const page of [host, friend]) {
120	    expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.net!.roster().length)).toBe(2);
121	  }
122	  await expect(host.getByRole("button", { name: /^Kick / })).toBeVisible(); // owner sees it
123	  expect(await friend.getByRole("button", { name: /^Kick / }).count()).toBe(0); // a member does not
124	
125	  // (Arrow replication — the `prj` channel — is covered by unit tests rather than
126	  // here: driving it end-to-end means creative-mode + inventory juggling + firing
127	  // into open sky to dodge the first-tick despawn, too fragile for a reliable e2e.
128	  // See server/room.test.ts (broadcast) and NetworkSession.test.ts (upsert).)
129	
130	  // ── the owner kicks the friend, who drops to the disconnect modal (LAST: it
131	  // tears down the friend's session). net.kick is exactly what the RosterPanel
132	  // Kick button's onClick calls.
133	  const friendId = await host.evaluate(() => {
134	    const net = window.__monecraft!.net!;
135	    return net.roster().find((member) => member.id !== net.playerId)!.id;
136	  });
137	  await host.evaluate((id) => window.__monecraft!.net!.kick(id), friendId);
138	  await expect.poll(() => friend.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.net!.status()), { timeout: 15000 }).toBe("closed");
139	  await expect(friend.getByRole("alertdialog", { name: "Disconnected" })).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 });
140	  await expect.poll(() => host.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.players.size), { timeout: 15000 }).toBe(1);
141	
142	  expect(errors, "no console/page errors during the test").toEqual([]);
143	  await hostContext.close();
144	  await friendContext.close();
145	});
146	
```

The friend arrives via invite as a guest, so the first-run screen now correctly shows the guest panel — its "Keep my worlds — create account" button collides with the non-exact `{ name: "Create" }` locator.   "content": "## What & why\n\nFixes two backwards / dead-end behaviors in the account onboarding:\n\n1. **Login was blocked behind profile creation.** On first run (before any local\n   profile exists) the menu rendered the create-profile form full-screen and only\n   mounted the account panel (Sign in / register / online) in the profile-list\n   view — so a brand-new player could not reach login/register until they'd first\n   created a local profile. The account panel now renders on the first-run screen\n   too.\n\n2. **Guest mode was a one-way street.** A guest was offered only \"Keep my worlds —\n   create account\", with no path back to the login screen. Guests now get a\n   **Sign out** button that returns to the offline state where Sign in / register\n   live.\n\nOffline-first is preserved: the account panel makes no auth request until this\nbrowser has used an online feature (the `onlineUsed()` gate), so a fresh offline\nplayer still triggers zero network calls.\n\n## Changes\n\n- `components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx` — render `<AccountPanel/>` on the first-run\n  create-profile screen.\n- `components/menu/AccountPanel.tsx` — extract a shared `signOut` helper; add a\n  Sign out button to the guest branch.\n- Unit tests for both components; the multiplayer e2e's profile `Create` locator\n  is disambiguated (a guest arriving via an invite link now also sees the account\n  panel on first run).\n- CHANGELOG entry + `docs/online.md` note.\n\n## Verification\n\n`bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`\n(1108 pass), `bun run build`, and e2e (menu, multiplayer, cloudSaves, smoke) all\ngreen locally.\n\n## Follow-up\n\nThis is Phase 1 of a larger identity rework (Local Players vs Accounts). Phases\n2–3 — server-side profiles, per-account/per-profile quotas, and retiring the\nanonymous-guest layer — land separately.\n"
}

Approved plan: `/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/multiplayer-is-already-working-zazzy-alpaca.md`.\n\n**Agreed model.** \"guest\" → renamed **Local Player** (logged out): many local (localStorage) profiles, local single-player worlds only, zero server calls, cannot create/join/invite online. **Account** (logged in): up to **5** server-side profiles (synced across devices), each owning up to **10** online worlds; can host/invite/join. On login, local worlds are **hidden-but-preserved** (never deleted, never auto-uploaded). The anonymous-guest server layer is deleted entirely — online == you have an account (a deliberate reversal of docs/online.md \"Identity: guests first\").\n\n**Phasing.**\n- **Phase 1** (the two reported bugs): login reachable on first-run screen + guest **Sign out**. DONE — branch `fix/account-menu-onboarding`, **PR #45** opened 2026-07-04, awaiting CI/CodeRabbit. Touched `components/menu/{ProfileSelect,AccountPanel}.tsx` (+tests), `e2e/multiplayer.e2e.ts` locator, CHANGELOG, docs/online.md.\n- **Phases 2+3 land together** on one branch `feat/local-vs-account-identity` → one PR (so prod never sees a half-migrated state). Phase 2: server `profiles` table + `worlds.profileId` + quotas (put `MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES=5`/`MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE=10` in config.ts) + gate online to non-anonymous in `lib/online/worldsService.ts` (surface `isAnonymous` in `lib/online/http.ts` `sessionUser`, which currently drops it) + auth-aware `ProfileSelect`. Phase 3: remove anonymous plugin/`onLinkAccount`/`ensureSignedIn`/`isAnonymous`, rewire `app/join/[token]` to require sign-in, migrate existing data, rewrite docs.\n\n**Open decisions:** save-slice keying (account `sub` vs `profileId`) for shared MP worlds; fate of existing deployed guest-owned online worlds (default: drop with a release-note warning).\n"

1	import { and, eq, isNull, lt, or, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
2	import type { Db } from "@/db";
3	import { schema } from "@/db";
4	import { WORLDGEN_VERSION } from "@/lib/game/config";
5	import { PROTOCOL_VERSION } from "@/lib/net/protocol";
6	import { signTicket } from "@/lib/net/tickets";
7	
8	/**
9	 * The online worlds domain: every rule the API routes enforce, as pure
10	 * functions over a drizzle Db — unit-tested against PGlite, adapted to HTTP by
11	 * the thin handlers in app/api. All read access is membership-gated; writes
12	 * that shape the world (rename/delete/invite) are owner-gated.
13	 *
14	 * Results use a discriminated `ok` so handlers map failures to status codes
15	 * without exceptions crossing the seam.
16	 */
17	
18	export type WorldSummary = {
19	  id: string;
20	  name: string;
21	  kind: "sp-cloud" | "mp";
22	  seed: number;
23	  worldType: string;
24	  gameMode: string;
25	  difficulty: string;
26	  hardcore: boolean;
27	  worldgenVersion: number;
28	  role: "owner" | "member";
29	  updatedAt: string;
30	};
31	
32	type Failure = { ok: false; error: "not-found" | "forbidden" | "conflict" | "invalid" | "expired" };
33	const fail = (error: Failure["error"]): Failure => ({ ok: false, error });
34	
35	function toSummary(world: typeof schema.worlds.$inferSelect, role: "owner" | "member"): WorldSummary {
36	  return {
37	    id: world.id,
38	    name: world.name,
39	    kind: world.kind,
40	    seed: world.seed,
41	    worldType: world.worldType,
42	    gameMode: world.gameMode,
43	    difficulty: world.difficulty,
44	    hardcore: world.hardcore,
45	    worldgenVersion: world.worldgenVersion,
46	    role,
47	    updatedAt: world.updatedAt.toISOString()
48	  };
49	}
50	
51	/** The caller's membership row for a world, or null. */
52	async function membership(db: Db, userId: string, worldId: string) {
53	  const rows = await db
54	    .select()
55	    .from(schema.worldMembers)
56	    .where(and(eq(schema.worldMembers.worldId, worldId), eq(schema.worldMembers.userId, userId)));
57	  return rows[0] ?? null;
58	}
59	
60	/** Every world the user may play, owner first then most recently updated. */
61	export async function listWorlds(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<WorldSummary[]> {
62	  const rows = await db
63	    .select({ world: schema.worlds, role: schema.worldMembers.role })
64	    .from(schema.worldMembers)
65	    .innerJoin(schema.worlds, eq(schema.worldMembers.worldId, schema.worlds.id))
66	    .where(eq(schema.worldMembers.userId, userId));
67	  return rows
68	    .map(({ world, role }) => toSummary(world, role))
69	    .sort((a, b) => (a.role !== b.role ? (a.role === "owner" ? -1 : 1) : b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt)));
70	}
71	
72	export type CreateWorldInput = {
73	  name: string;
74	  kind: "sp-cloud" | "mp";
75	  seed: number;
76	  worldType?: string;
77	  gameMode?: string;
78	  difficulty?: string;
79	  hardcore?: boolean;
80	};
81	
82	export async function createWorld(db: Db, userId: string, input: CreateWorldInput): Promise<{ ok: true; world: WorldSummary } | Failure> {
83	  const name = input.name?.trim();
84	  if (!name || name.length > 64) return fail("invalid");
85	  if (input.kind !== "sp-cloud" && input.kind !== "mp") return fail("invalid");
86	  if (!Number.isFinite(input.seed)) return fail("invalid");
87	  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
88	  const [world] = await db
89	    .insert(schema.worlds)
90	    .values({
91	      id,
92	      ownerId: userId,
93	      kind: input.kind,
94	      name,
95	      seed: Math.floor(input.seed),
96	      worldType: input.worldType ?? "default",
97	      gameMode: input.gameMode ?? "survival",
98	      difficulty: input.difficulty ?? "normal",
99	      hardcore: input.hardcore ?? false,
100	      worldgenVersion: WORLDGEN_VERSION
101	    })
102	    .returning();
103	  await db.insert(schema.worldMembers).values({ worldId: id, userId, role: "owner" });
104	  return { ok: true, world: toSummary(world, "owner") };
105	}
106	
107	export async function getWorld(db: Db, userId: string, worldId: string): Promise<{ ok: true; world: WorldSummary } | Failure> {
108	  const member = await membership(db, userId, worldId);
109	  if (!member) return fail("not-found"); // membership-gated: outsiders can't probe ids
110	  const [world] = await db.select().from(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.id, worldId));
111	  if (!world) return fail("not-found");
112	  return { ok: true, world: toSummary(world, member.role) };
113	}
114	
115	export async function renameWorld(db: Db, userId: string, worldId: string, name: string): Promise<{ ok: true } | Failure> {
116	  const trimmed = name?.trim();
117	  if (!trimmed || trimmed.length > 64) return fail("invalid");
118	  const member = await membership(db, userId, worldId);
119	  if (!member) return fail("not-found");
120	  if (member.role !== "owner") return fail("forbidden");
121	  await db.update(schema.worlds).set({ name: trimmed, updatedAt: new Date() }).where(eq(schema.worlds.id, worldId));
122	  return { ok: true };
123	}
124	
125	export async function deleteWorld(db: Db, userId: string, worldId: string): Promise<{ ok: true } | Failure> {
126	  const member = await membership(db, userId, worldId);
127	  if (!member) return fail("not-found");
128	  if (member.role !== "owner") return fail("forbidden");
129	  await db.delete(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.id, worldId)); // members/invites cascade
130	  return { ok: true };
131	}
132	
133	/** The gzipped SaveData blob plus its concurrency stamp. */
134	export async function getSaveBlob(
135	  db: Db,
136	  userId: string,
137	  worldId: string
138	): Promise<{ ok: true; blob: Uint8Array | null; saveVersion: number | null; updatedAt: string } | Failure> {
139	  const member = await membership(db, userId, worldId);
140	  if (!member) return fail("not-found");
141	  const [world] = await db.select().from(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.id, worldId));
142	  if (!world) return fail("not-found");
143	  return { ok: true, blob: world.saveBlob ?? null, saveVersion: world.saveVersion, updatedAt: world.updatedAt.toISOString() };
144	}
145	
146	/**
147	 * Last-write-wins with a stale guard: the client sends the `updatedAt` it
148	 * last saw; a mismatch means someone else (another device) wrote in between —
149	 * 409, and the client picks the newer save. `baseUpdatedAt: null` means
150	 * "first upload", accepted only while the world has no blob yet.
151	 */
152	export async function putSaveBlob(
153	  db: Db,
154	  userId: string,
155	  worldId: string,
156	  blob: Uint8Array,
157	  saveVersion: number,
158	  baseUpdatedAt: string | null
159	): Promise<{ ok: true; updatedAt: string } | Failure> {
160	  const member = await membership(db, userId, worldId);
161	  if (!member) return fail("not-found");
162	  if (!Number.isInteger(saveVersion) || blob.byteLength === 0) return fail("invalid");
163	  const now = new Date();
164	  // Fold the stale check into the UPDATE predicate so two writers with the same
165	  // base stamp can't both win: first upload requires no blob yet; a later one
166	  // requires the exact `updatedAt` it last saw. Zero rows affected = stale.
167	  const guard =
168	    baseUpdatedAt === null
169	      ? and(eq(schema.worlds.id, worldId), isNull(schema.worlds.saveBlob))
170	      : and(eq(schema.worlds.id, worldId), eq(schema.worlds.updatedAt, new Date(baseUpdatedAt)));
171	  const updated = await db.update(schema.worlds).set({ saveBlob: blob, saveVersion, updatedAt: now }).where(guard).returning({ id: schema.worlds.id });
172	  // Membership implies the world exists (FK cascade), so no rows means a stale base.
173	  if (updated.length === 0) return fail("conflict");
174	  return { ok: true, updatedAt: now.toISOString() };
175	}
176	
177	export async function createInvite(db: Db, userId: string, worldId: string): Promise<{ ok: true; token: string } | Failure> {
178	  const member = await membership(db, userId, worldId);
179	  if (!member) return fail("not-found");
180	  if (member.role !== "owner") return fail("forbidden");
181	  const token = base64urlToken();
182	  await db.insert(schema.worldInvites).values({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), worldId, token, createdBy: userId });
183	  return { ok: true, token };
184	}
185	
186	/**
187	 * Owner revokes every outstanding invite for a world — a shared link that
188	 * escaped is instantly dead (existing members keep their access). Returns how
189	 * many links were killed so the UI can confirm.
190	 */
191	export async function revokeInvites(db: Db, userId: string, worldId: string): Promise<{ ok: true; revoked: number } | Failure> {
192	  const member = await membership(db, userId, worldId);
193	  if (!member) return fail("not-found");
194	  if (member.role !== "owner") return fail("forbidden");
195	  const killed = await db.delete(schema.worldInvites).where(eq(schema.worldInvites.worldId, worldId)).returning({ id: schema.worldInvites.id });
196	  return { ok: true, revoked: killed.length };
197	}
198	
199	/** 128 bits of URL-safe randomness. */
200	function base64urlToken(): string {
201	  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
202	  let binary = "";
203	  for (const b of bytes) binary += String.fromCharCode(b);
204	  return btoa(binary).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
205	}
206	
207	export async function resolveInvite(db: Db, token: string): Promise<{ ok: true; worldId: string; worldName: string } | Failure> {
208	  const [invite] = await db.select().from(schema.worldInvites).where(eq(schema.worldInvites.token, token));
209	  if (!invite) return fail("not-found");
210	  if (invite.expiresAt && invite.expiresAt.getTime() < Date.now()) return fail("expired");
211	  if (invite.maxUses !== null && invite.uses >= invite.maxUses) return fail("expired");
212	  const [world] = await db.select().from(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.id, invite.worldId));
213	  if (!world) return fail("not-found");
214	  return { ok: true, worldId: world.id, worldName: world.name };
215	}
216	
217	/** Accepting twice is fine (idempotent); each NEW membership consumes one use. */
218	export async function acceptInvite(db: Db, userId: string, token: string): Promise<{ ok: true; worldId: string } | Failure> {
219	  const resolved = await resolveInvite(db, token);
220	  if (!resolved.ok) return resolved;
221	  const existing = await membership(db, userId, resolved.worldId);
222	  if (existing) return { ok: true, worldId: resolved.worldId };
223	  // Consume one use atomically, enforcing the cap in the predicate: concurrent
224	  // accepts can't both slip past a maxUses that only one increment should clear.
225	  const consumed = await db
226	    .update(schema.worldInvites)
227	    .set({ uses: sql`${schema.worldInvites.uses} + 1` })
228	    .where(and(eq(schema.worldInvites.token, token), or(isNull(schema.worldInvites.maxUses), lt(schema.worldInvites.uses, schema.worldInvites.maxUses))))
229	    .returning({ id: schema.worldInvites.id });
230	  if (consumed.length === 0) return fail("expired"); // the cap filled under concurrency
231	  await db.insert(schema.worldMembers).values({ worldId: resolved.worldId, userId, role: "member" }).onConflictDoNothing();
232	  return { ok: true, worldId: resolved.worldId };
233	}
234	
235	/** Mints the 60s join ticket the game server trusts. Membership required; mp worlds only. */
236	export async function mintTicket(
237	  db: Db,
238	  user: { id: string; name: string; skinId?: string | null },
239	  worldId: string,
240	  secret: string
241	): Promise<{ ok: true; ticket: string } | Failure> {
242	  const member = await membership(db, user.id, worldId);
243	  if (!member) return fail("not-found");
244	  const [world] = await db.select().from(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.id, worldId));
245	  if (!world) return fail("not-found");
246	  if (world.kind !== "mp") return fail("invalid");
247	  const ticket = await signTicket(
248	    { sub: user.id, wid: worldId, name: user.name, skinId: user.skinId ?? null, role: member.role, pv: PROTOCOL_VERSION },
249	    secret
250	  );
251	  return { ok: true, ticket };
252	}
253	
```

1	import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js";
2	import postgres from "postgres";
3	import * as schema from "./schema";
4	
5	/**
6	 * The Postgres connection, lazily created so importing this module never
7	 * requires DATABASE_URL (the client bundle types against the schema; only
8	 * route handlers and the game server actually connect). postgres-js runs on
9	 * both Node (Vercel functions) and Bun (the Fly game server); tests use the
10	 * PGlite driver via db/testDb.ts instead.
11	 *
12	 * `DATABASE_URL=pglite://memory` runs an EPHEMERAL in-process Postgres with
13	 * the schema pre-applied — the Playwright multiplayer suite and daemon-free
14	 * local dev use it; data lives exactly as long as the server process.
15	 */
16	
17	export type Db = ReturnType<typeof drizzle<typeof schema>>;
18	
19	let instance: Db | null = null;
20	
21	export function db(): Db {
22	  if (instance) return instance;
23	  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL;
24	  if (!url) throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is not set — see .env.example");
25	  if (url.startsWith("pglite:")) {
26	    // Lazy requires keep PGlite (a WASM bundle) out of production paths.
27	    /* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports */
28	    const { PGlite } = require("@electric-sql/pglite") as typeof import("@electric-sql/pglite");
29	    const { drizzle: drizzlePglite } = require("drizzle-orm/pglite") as typeof import("drizzle-orm/pglite");
30	    const { SCHEMA_DDL } = require("./ddl") as typeof import("./ddl");
31	    /* eslint-enable @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports */
32	    const client = new PGlite();
33	    // exec() is async; PGlite queues queries behind it, so no race. Surface a
34	    // schema-init failure loudly instead of leaving it an unhandled rejection.
35	    void client.exec(SCHEMA_DDL).catch((error: unknown) => {
36	      console.error("pglite schema init failed", error);
37	    });
38	    instance = drizzlePglite(client, { schema }) as unknown as Db;
39	    return instance;
40	  }
41	  // Vercel Fluid Compute reuses instances; keep the pool tiny per instance.
42	  instance = drizzle(postgres(url, { max: 5 }), { schema });
43	  return instance;
44	}
45	
46	export { schema };
47	
```

4	/**
5	 * Shared plumbing for the online API routes: cookie-session extraction and
6	 * the mapping from worldsService's typed failures to HTTP status codes. The
7	 * routes stay one-screen adapters; the rules live (tested) in worldsService.
8	 */
9	
10	export type SessionUser = { id: string; name: string; skinId: string | null };
11	
12	/** The signed-in user, or null (routes answer 401). */
13	export async function sessionUser(request: Request): Promise<SessionUser | null> {
14	  const session = await auth().api.getSession({ headers: request.headers as Headers });
15	  if (!session?.user) return null;
16	  const user = session.user as { id: string; name: string; skinId?: string | null };
17	  return { id: user.id, name: user.name, skinId: user.skinId ?? null };
18	}
19	
20	export const unauthorized = (): NextResponse => NextResponse.json({ error: "unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

22	const STATUS: Record<string, number> = {
23	  "not-found": 404,
24	  forbidden: 403,
25	  conflict: 409,
26	  invalid: 400,
27	  expired: 410
28	};
29	
30	export function failureResponse(error: string): NextResponse {
31	  return NextResponse.json({ error }, { status: STATUS[error] ?? 500 });
32	}

```
1	/**
2	 * Join tickets: the only trust link between the web app and the game server.
3	 *
4	 * The Next.js API (which can read the browser's session cookie) mints a
5	 * short-lived HS256 token naming the player and the world; the game server
6	 * (which never sees cookies) verifies the signature with the shared
7	 * GAME_TICKET_SECRET and admits the socket. Stateless by design — the two
8	 * deployments never talk to each other at runtime.
9	 *
10	 * Web Crypto only (no jwt dependency): runs identically on Vercel's Node
11	 * runtime, Bun, and the browser-adjacent test environments.
12	 */
13	
14	export const TICKET_TTL_SECONDS = 60;
15	
16	export type TicketClaims = {
17	  /** The player's user id (becomes their PlayerId in the world). */
18	  sub: string;
19	  /** The world (= room) this ticket admits into. */
20	  wid: string;
21	  /** Display name at mint time. */
22	  name: string;
23	  /** Skin palette id, if the account has one. */
24	  skinId: string | null;
25	  /** Room role — the owner may kick/close. */
26	  role: "owner" | "member";
27	  /** Protocol version the client was built against. */
28	  pv: number;
29	  iat: number;
30	  exp: number;
31	};
32	
33	const encoder = new TextEncoder();
34	
35	function base64url(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
36	  let binary = "";
37	  for (const b of bytes) binary += String.fromCharCode(b);
38	  return btoa(binary).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
39	}
40	
41	function fromBase64url(text: string): Uint8Array {
42	  const padded = text
43	    .replace(/-/g, "+")
44	    .replace(/_/g, "/")
45	    .padEnd(Math.ceil(text.length / 4) * 4, "=");
46	  const binary = atob(padded);
47	  const out = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
48	  for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i += 1) out[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
49	  return out;
50	}
51	
52	async function hmacKey(secret: string): Promise<CryptoKey> {
53	  return crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", encoder.encode(secret), { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" }, false, ["sign", "verify"]);
54	}
55	
56	/** Mints a signed ticket. `now` is injectable for expiry tests. */
57	export async function signTicket(claims: Omit<TicketClaims, "iat" | "exp">, secret: string, now: number = Date.now()): Promise<string> {
58	  const iat = Math.floor(now / 1000);
59	  const full: TicketClaims = { ...claims, iat, exp: iat + TICKET_TTL_SECONDS };
60	  const header = base64url(encoder.encode(JSON.stringify({ alg: "HS256", typ: "JWT" })));
61	  const payload = base64url(encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(full)));
62	  const signature = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", await hmacKey(secret), encoder.encode(`${header}.${payload}`));
63	  return `${header}.${payload}.${base64url(new Uint8Array(signature))}`;
64	}
65	
66	/**
67	 * Verifies signature and expiry, returning the claims — or null for anything
68	 * malformed, tampered, or stale. Total: never throws on hostile input.
69	 */
70	export async function verifyTicket(ticket: string, secret: string, now: number = Date.now()): Promise<TicketClaims | null> {
71	  try {
72	    const parts = ticket.split(".");
73	    if (parts.length !== 3) return null;
74	    const [header, payload, signature] = parts;
75	    const valid = await crypto.subtle.verify(
76	      "HMAC",
77	      await hmacKey(secret),
78	      fromBase64url(signature) as unknown as ArrayBuffer,
79	      encoder.encode(`${header}.${payload}`)
80	    );
81	    if (!valid) return null;
82	    const claims = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(fromBase64url(payload))) as TicketClaims;
83	    if (typeof claims.sub !== "string" || !claims.sub) return null;
84	    if (typeof claims.wid !== "string" || !claims.wid) return null;
85	    if (claims.role !== "owner" && claims.role !== "member") return null;
86	    if (typeof claims.pv !== "number") return null;
87	    if (typeof claims.exp !== "number" || Math.floor(now / 1000) >= claims.exp) return null;
88	    return claims;
89	  } catch {
90	    return null;
91	  }
92	}
93	
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/db/schema.ts",
  "old_string": "// ── game tables ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * One world. `kind` separates a cloud-synced single-player world (the blob is\n * the client's latest upload) from a multiplayer world (the blob is the game\n * server's authoritative persistence). One save blob either way: gzipped\n * SaveData JSON (v17 carries every player's slice).\n */\nexport const worlds = pgTable(\"worlds\", {\n  id: text(\"id\").primaryKey(),\n  ownerId: text(\"owner_id\")\n    .notNull()\n    .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),",
  "new_string": "// ── game tables ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * A player profile owned by an account: the cross-device identity (name +\n * skin) that owns online worlds. Local Players keep their profiles in the\n * browser (lib/game/profiles.ts); these are the server-side ones an account\n * syncs. Capped per account (config MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES).\n */\nexport const profiles = pgTable(\"profiles\", {\n  id: text(\"id\").primaryKey(),\n  ownerId: text(\"owner_id\")\n    .notNull()\n    .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),\n  name: text(\"name\").notNull(),\n  /** Skin palette id (mirrors the local Profile's skinId). */\n  skinId: text(\"skin_id\"),\n  createdAt: timestamp(\"created_at\").notNull().defaultNow()\n});\n\n/**\n * One world. `kind` separates a cloud-synced single-player world (the blob is\n * the client's latest upload) from a multiplayer world (the blob is the game\n * server's authoritative persistence). One save blob either way: gzipped\n * SaveData JSON (v17 carries every player's slice).\n */\nexport const worlds = pgTable(\"worlds\", {\n  id: text(\"id\").primaryKey(),\n  ownerId: text(\"owner_id\")\n    .notNull()\n    .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),\n  /**\n   * Which of the owner's profiles owns this world. Nullable: pre-profile rows\n   * (and the account's default profile backfill) leave it null until claimed.\n   */\n  profileId: text(\"profile_id\").references(() => profiles.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),"
}
```

Anonymous guests are skipped —\n-- that layer is being retired and their throwaway worlds are not carried over.\nINSERT INTO \"profiles\" (\"id\", \"owner_id\", \"name\", \"skin_id\", \"created_at\")\nSELECT gen_random_uuid(), \"id\", \"name\", \"skin_id\", now() FROM \"user\" WHERE \"is_anonymous\" IS NOT TRUE;--> statement-breakpoint\nUPDATE \"worlds\" w SET \"profile_id\" = p.\"id\" FROM \"profiles\" p WHERE p.\"owner_id\" = w.\"owner_id\" AND w.\"profile_id\" IS NULL;"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/online/worldsService.ts",
  "old_string": "    hardcore: world.hardcore,\n    worldgenVersion: world.worldgenVersion,\n    role,\n    updatedAt: world.updatedAt.toISOString()\n  };\n}",
  "new_string": "    hardcore: world.hardcore,\n    worldgenVersion: world.worldgenVersion,\n    role,\n    profileId: world.profileId ?? null,\n    updatedAt: world.updatedAt.toISOString()\n  };\n}\n\n// ── account profiles ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport type ProfileSummary = { id: string; name: string; skinId: string | null; createdAt: string };\n\nconst MAX_PROFILE_NAME = 24;\n\nfunction toProfileSummary(profile: typeof schema.profiles.$inferSelect): ProfileSummary {\n  return { id: profile.id, name: profile.name, skinId: profile.skinId ?? null, createdAt: profile.createdAt.toISOString() };\n}\n\n/** Every profile an account owns, oldest first (stable menu order). */\nexport async function listProfiles(db: Db, ownerId: string): Promise<ProfileSummary[]> {\n  const rows = await db.select().from(schema.profiles).where(eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, ownerId));\n  return rows.map(toProfileSummary).sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));\n}\n\n/**\n * Creates an account profile. Guests can't (online identities are account-only,\n * see [[Local Player]]); the account is capped at MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES — a 6th\n * request is a `conflict` the UI reports as \"limit reached\".\n */\nexport async function createProfile(\n  db: Db,\n  owner: { id: string; isAnonymous: boolean },\n  input: { name: string; skinId?: string | null }\n): Promise<{ ok: true; profile: ProfileSummary } | Failure> {\n  if (owner.isAnonymous) return fail(\"forbidden\");\n  const name = input.name?.trim();\n  if (!name || name.length > MAX_PROFILE_NAME) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  const existing = await db.select({ id: schema.profiles.id }).from(schema.profiles).where(eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, owner.id));\n  if (existing.length >= MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES) return fail(\"conflict\");\n  const [profile] = await db\n    .insert(schema.profiles)\n    .values({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), ownerId: owner.id, name, skinId: input.skinId ?? null })\n    .returning();\n  return { ok: true, profile: toProfileSummary(profile) };\n}\n\n/** Rename and/or reskin a profile (owner-scoped). */\nexport async function updateProfile(\n  db: Db,\n  ownerId: string,\n  profileId: string,\n  patch: { name?: string; skinId?: string | null }\n): Promise<{ ok: true } | Failure> {\n  const set: { name?: string; skinId?: string | null } = {};\n  if (patch.name !== undefined) {\n    const trimmed = patch.name.trim();\n    if (!trimmed || trimmed.length > MAX_PROFILE_NAME) return fail(\"invalid\");\n    set.name = trimmed;\n  }\n  if (patch.skinId !== undefined) set.skinId = patch.skinId;\n  if (Object.keys(set).length === 0) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  const updated = await db\n    .update(schema.profiles)\n    .set(set)\n    .where(and(eq(schema.profiles.id, profileId), eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, ownerId)))\n    .returning({ id: schema.profiles.id });\n  if (updated.length === 0) return fail(\"not-found\");\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\n/** Deletes a profile and (via FK cascade) all the online worlds it owned. */\nexport async function deleteProfile(db: Db, ownerId: string, profileId: string): Promise<{ ok: true } | Failure> {\n  const deleted = await db\n    .delete(schema.profiles)\n    .where(and(eq(schema.profiles.id, profileId), eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, ownerId)))\n    .returning({ id: schema.profiles.id });\n  if (deleted.length === 0) return fail(\"not-found\");\n  return { ok: true };\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/online/worldsService.ts",
  "old_string": "export type CreateWorldInput = {\n  name: string;\n  kind: \"sp-cloud\" | \"mp\";\n  seed: number;\n  worldType?: string;\n  gameMode?: string;\n  difficulty?: string;\n  hardcore?: boolean;\n};\n\nexport async function createWorld(db: Db, userId: string, input: CreateWorldInput): Promise<{ ok: true; world: WorldSummary } | Failure> {\n  const name = input.name?.trim();\n  if (!name || name.length > 64) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  if (input.kind !== \"sp-cloud\" && input.kind !== \"mp\") return fail(\"invalid\");\n  if (!Number.isFinite(input.seed)) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  const id = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const [world] = await db\n    .insert(schema.worlds)\n    .values({\n      id,\n      ownerId: userId,\n      kind: input.kind,\n      name,\n      seed: Math.floor(input.seed),\n      worldType: input.worldType ?? \"default\",\n      gameMode: input.gameMode ?? \"survival\",\n      difficulty: input.difficulty ?? \"normal\",\n      hardcore: input.hardcore ?? false,\n      worldgenVersion: WORLDGEN_VERSION\n    })\n    .returning();\n  await db.insert(schema.worldMembers).values({ worldId: id, userId, role: \"owner\" });\n  return { ok: true, world: toSummary(world, \"owner\") };\n}",
  "new_string": "export type CreateWorldInput = {\n  name: string;\n  kind: \"sp-cloud\" | \"mp\";\n  seed: number;\n  worldType?: string;\n  gameMode?: string;\n  difficulty?: string;\n  hardcore?: boolean;\n  /** The owner's profile that owns this world; enforces the per-profile world cap. */\n  profileId?: string;\n};\n\nexport async function createWorld(db: Db, userId: string, input: CreateWorldInput): Promise<{ ok: true; world: WorldSummary } | Failure> {\n  const name = input.name?.trim();\n  if (!name || name.length > 64) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  if (input.kind !== \"sp-cloud\" && input.kind !== \"mp\") return fail(\"invalid\");\n  if (!Number.isFinite(input.seed)) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  let profileId: string | null = null;\n  if (input.profileId) {\n    // The profile must be the caller's, and it can't already be at its world cap.\n    const [profile] = await db\n      .select({ id: schema.profiles.id })\n      .from(schema.profiles)\n      .where(and(eq(schema.profiles.id, input.profileId), eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, userId)));\n    if (!profile) return fail(\"forbidden\");\n    const owned = await db.select({ id: schema.worlds.id }).from(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.profileId, input.profileId));\n    if (owned.length >= MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE) return fail(\"conflict\");\n    profileId = input.profileId;\n  }\n  const id = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const [world] = await db\n    .insert(schema.worlds)\n    .values({\n      id,\n      ownerId: userId,\n      profileId,\n      kind: input.kind,\n      name,\n      seed: Math.floor(input.seed),\n      worldType: input.worldType ?? \"default\",\n      gameMode: input.gameMode ?? \"survival\",\n      difficulty: input.difficulty ?? \"normal\",\n      hardcore: input.hardcore ?? false,\n      worldgenVersion: WORLDGEN_VERSION\n    })\n    .returning();\n  await db.insert(schema.worldMembers).values({ worldId: id, userId, role: \"owner\" });\n  return { ok: true, world: toSummary(world, \"owner\") };\n}"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/online/worldsService.ts",
  "old_string": "/** Mints the 60s join ticket the game server trusts. Membership required; mp worlds only. */\nexport async function mintTicket(\n  db: Db,\n  user: { id: string; name: string; skinId?: string | null },\n  worldId: string,\n  secret: string\n): Promise<{ ok: true; ticket: string } | Failure> {\n  const member = await membership(db, user.id, worldId);\n  if (!member) return fail(\"not-found\");\n  const [world] = await db.select().from(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.id, worldId));\n  if (!world) return fail(\"not-found\");\n  if (world.kind !== \"mp\") return fail(\"invalid\");\n  const ticket = await signTicket(\n    { sub: user.id, wid: worldId, name: user.name, skinId: user.skinId ?? null, role: member.role, pv: PROTOCOL_VERSION },\n    secret\n  );\n  return { ok: true, ticket };\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Mints the 60s join ticket the game server trusts. Membership required; mp\n * worlds only. When a `profileId` is given (and belongs to the caller) the\n * ticket carries that profile's name + skin, so others see the profile identity\n * — not the account. `sub` stays the account id (roster/kick key at the account\n * level; per-profile save-slice keying is a separate decision).\n */\nexport async function mintTicket(\n  db: Db,\n  user: { id: string; name: string; skinId?: string | null },\n  worldId: string,\n  secret: string,\n  profileId?: string | null\n): Promise<{ ok: true; ticket: string } | Failure> {\n  const member = await membership(db, user.id, worldId);\n  if (!member) return fail(\"not-found\");\n  const [world] = await db.select().from(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.id, worldId));\n  if (!world) return fail(\"not-found\");\n  if (world.kind !== \"mp\") return fail(\"invalid\");\n  let name = user.name;\n  let skinId = user.skinId ?? null;\n  if (profileId) {\n    const [profile] = await db\n      .select()\n      .from(schema.profiles)\n      .where(and(eq(schema.profiles.id, profileId), eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, user.id)));\n    if (!profile) return fail(\"forbidden\");\n    name = profile.name;\n    skinId = profile.skinId ?? null;\n  }\n  const ticket = await signTicket({ sub: user.id, wid: worldId, name, skinId, role: member.role, pv: PROTOCOL_VERSION }, secret);\n  return { ok: true, ticket };\n}"
}

  "new_string": "export type SessionUser = { id: string; name: string; skinId: string | null; isAnonymous: boolean };\n\n/** The signed-in user, or null (routes answer 401). */\nexport async function sessionUser(request: Request): Promise<SessionUser | null> {\n  const session = await auth().api.getSession({ headers: request.headers as Headers });\n  if (!session?.user) return null;\n  const user = session.user as { id: string; name: string; skinId?: string | null; isAnonymous?: boolean | null };\n  return { id: user.id, name: user.name, skinId: user.skinId ?? null, isAnonymous: user.isAnonymous === true };\n}"
}

1	import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
2	import { db } from "@/db";
3	import { failureResponse, sessionUser, unauthorized } from "@/lib/online/http";
4	import { createWorld, listWorlds, type CreateWorldInput } from "@/lib/online/worldsService";
5	
6	/** GET /api/worlds — every world the signed-in user may play. */
7	export async function GET(request: Request) {
8	  const user = await sessionUser(request);
9	  if (!user) return unauthorized();
10	  return NextResponse.json({ worlds: await listWorlds(db(), user.id) });
11	}
12	
13	/** POST /api/worlds — create a world (sp-cloud or mp); the creator becomes owner. */
14	export async function POST(request: Request) {
15	  const user = await sessionUser(request);
16	  if (!user) return unauthorized();
17	  const body = (await request.json().catch(() => null)) as CreateWorldInput | null;
18	  if (!body) return failureResponse("invalid");
19	  const result = await createWorld(db(), user.id, body);
20	  if (!result.ok) return failureResponse(result.error);
21	  return NextResponse.json({ world: result.world }, { status: 201 });
22	}
23

1	import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
2	import { db } from "@/db";
3	import { failureResponse, sessionUser, unauthorized } from "@/lib/online/http";
4	import { mintTicket } from "@/lib/online/worldsService";
5	
6	type Params = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };
7	
8	/**
9	 * POST /api/worlds/:id/ticket — mint the 60-second join ticket the game
10	 * server admits sockets with. The only endpoint that touches the shared
11	 * GAME_TICKET_SECRET; the game server itself never sees a cookie.
12	 */
13	export async function POST(request: Request, { params }: Params) {
14	  const user = await sessionUser(request);
15	  if (!user) return unauthorized();
16	  const secret = process.env.GAME_TICKET_SECRET;
17	  if (!secret) return NextResponse.json({ error: "server-misconfigured" }, { status: 500 });
18	  const { id } = await params;
19	  const result = await mintTicket(db(), user, id, secret);
20	  if (!result.ok) return failureResponse(result.error);
21	  return NextResponse.json({ ticket: result.ticket, gameServerUrl: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GAME_SERVER_URL ?? null });
22	}
23

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/app/api/profiles/route.ts",
  "content": "import { NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\nimport { db } from \"@/db\";\nimport { failureResponse, sessionUser, unauthorized } from \"@/lib/online/http\";\nimport { createProfile, listProfiles } from \"@/lib/online/worldsService\";\n\n/** GET /api/profiles — the signed-in account's online profiles. */\nexport async function GET(request: Request) {\n  const user = await sessionUser(request);\n  if (!user) return unauthorized();\n  return NextResponse.json({ profiles: await listProfiles(db(), user.id) });\n}\n\n/** POST /api/profiles — create an account profile (guests can't; capped per account). */\nexport async function POST(request: Request) {\n  const user = await sessionUser(request);\n  if (!user) return unauthorized();\n  const body = (await request.json().catch(() => null)) as { name?: string; skinId?: string | null } | null;\n  if (!body) return failureResponse(\"invalid\");\n  const result = await createProfile(db(), { id: user.id, isAnonymous: user.isAnonymous }, { name: body.name ?? \"\", skinId: body.skinId ?? null });\n  if (!result.ok) return failureResponse(result.error);\n  return NextResponse.json({ profile: result.profile }, { status: 201 });\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/online/profilesClient.ts",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport type { ProfileSummary } from \"@/lib/online/worldsService\";\n\n/**\n * Browser-side fetch helpers for account (online) profiles — the cross-device\n * identities a signed-in account owns. Thin and total: failures come back as\n * null/false, never exceptions, so the menu shows offline/limit states instead\n * of crashing. Local Players use lib/game/profiles.ts (localStorage) instead.\n */\n\nexport type OnlineProfile = ProfileSummary;\n\nexport async function listOnlineProfiles(): Promise<OnlineProfile[] | null> {\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(\"/api/profiles\");\n    if (!response.ok) return null;\n    const { profiles } = (await response.json()) as { profiles: OnlineProfile[] };\n    return profiles;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function createOnlineProfile(input: { name: string; skinId?: string | null }): Promise<OnlineProfile | null> {\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(\"/api/profiles\", {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" },\n      body: JSON.stringify(input)\n    });\n    if (!response.ok) return null;\n    const { profile } = (await response.json()) as { profile: OnlineProfile };\n    return profile;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/** Rename and/or reskin a profile. Returns whether it succeeded. */\nexport async function updateOnlineProfile(id: string, patch: { name?: string; skinId?: string | null }): Promise<boolean> {\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`/api/profiles/${id}`, {\n      method: \"PATCH\",\n      headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" },\n      body: JSON.stringify(patch)\n    });\n    return response.ok;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\n/** Delete a profile and (server-side, cascading) its online worlds. */\nexport async function deleteOnlineProfile(id: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`/api/profiles/${id}`, { method: \"DELETE\" });\n    return response.ok;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n"
}

2	import { closeTestDb, createTestDb, type TestDb } from "@/db/testDb";
3	import { schema } from "@/db";
4	import { verifyTicket } from "@/lib/net/tickets";
5	import {
6	  acceptInvite,
7	  createInvite,
8	  createWorld,
9	  deleteWorld,
10	  getSaveBlob,
11	  getWorld,
12	  listWorlds,
