14	  putSaveBlob,
15	  renameWorld,
16	  resolveInvite,
17	  revokeInvites
18	} from "./worldsService";
19	
20	let db: TestDb;
21	const asDb = () => db as never;
22	
23	async function addUser(id: string): Promise<void> {
24	  await db.insert(schema.user).values({ id, name: id, email: `${id}@example.com` });
25	}
26	
27	beforeEach(async () => {
28	  db = await createTestDb();
29	  await addUser("alice");
30	  await addUser("bob");
31	  await addUser("mallory");
32	});
33	
34	// An unclosed PGlite leaks a pending WASM op that Bun flags as exit code 99.
35	afterEach(async () => {
36	  await closeTestDb(db);
37	});
38	
39	async function makeWorld(owner = "alice", kind: "sp-cloud" | "mp" = "mp"): Promise<string> {
40	  const result = await createWorld(asDb(), owner, { name: "Test World", kind, seed: 1337 });
41	  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.error);
42	  return result.world.id;
43	}
44	
45	describe("worlds CRUD", () => {
46	  test("create gives the owner a membership and shows up in their list", async () => {
47	    const id = await makeWorld();
48	    const list = await listWorlds(asDb(), "alice");
49	    expect(list.map((w) => w.id)).toEqual([id]);
50	    expect(list[0].role).toBe("owner");
51	    expect(list[0].worldgenVersion).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(11);
52	  });
53	
54	  test("outsiders can't see, rename, or delete a world (membership-gated as not-found)", async () => {
55	    const id = await makeWorld();
56	    expect((await getWorld(asDb(), "mallory", id)).ok).toBe(false);
57	    expect(await renameWorld(asDb(), "mallory", id, "Stolen")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "not-found" });
58	    expect(await deleteWorld(asDb(), "mallory", id)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "not-found" });
59	  });
60	
61	  test("members can read but only the owner mutates", async () => {
62	    const id = await makeWorld();
63	    const invite = await createInvite(asDb(), "alice", id);
64	    if (!invite.ok) throw new Error("invite failed");
65	    await acceptInvite(asDb(), "bob", invite.token);
66	
67	    expect((await getWorld(asDb(), "bob", id)).ok).toBe(true);
68	    expect(await renameWorld(asDb(), "bob", id, "Bob's Now")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "forbidden" });
69	    expect(await createInvite(asDb(), "bob", id)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "forbidden" });
70	    expect(await renameWorld(asDb(), "alice", id, "Renamed")).toMatchObject({ ok: true });
71	  });
72	
73	  test("rejects garbage world creation", async () => {
74	    expect(await createWorld(asDb(), "alice", { name: "  ", kind: "mp", seed: 1 })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "invalid" });
75	    expect(await createWorld(asDb(), "alice", { name: "x".repeat(65), kind: "mp", seed: 1 })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "invalid" });
76	    expect(await createWorld(asDb(), "alice", { name: "ok", kind: "nope" as never, seed: 1 })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "invalid" });
77	    expect(await createWorld(asDb(), "alice", { name: "ok", kind: "mp", seed: Number.NaN })).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "invalid" });
78	  });
79	});
80	
81	describe("invites", () => {
82	  test("accept is idempotent and consumes uses only for new members", async () => {
83	    const id = await makeWorld();
84	    const invite = await createInvite(asDb(), "alice", id);
85	    if (!invite.ok) throw new Error("invite failed");
86	
87	    expect(await acceptInvite(asDb(), "bob", invite.token)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, worldId: id });
88	    expect(await acceptInvite(asDb(), "bob", invite.token)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, worldId: id });
89	
90	    const [row] = await db.select().from(schema.worldInvites);
91	    expect(row.uses).toBe(1); // the second accept was a no-op
92	
93	    const list = await listWorlds(asDb(), "bob");
94	    expect(list.map((w) => w.id)).toEqual([id]);
95	    expect(list[0].role).toBe("member");
96	  });
97	
98	  test("expired and exhausted invites refuse", async () => {
99	    const id = await makeWorld();
100	    await db.insert(schema.worldInvites).values({ id: "inv-old", worldId: id, token: "tok-old", createdBy: "alice", expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000) });
101	    expect(await resolveInvite(asDb(), "tok-old")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "expired" });
102	
103	    await db.insert(schema.worldInvites).values({ id: "inv-max", worldId: id, token: "tok-max", createdBy: "alice", maxUses: 1, uses: 1 });
104	    expect(await acceptInvite(asDb(), "bob", "tok-max")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "expired" });
105	
106	    expect(await resolveInvite(asDb(), "tok-nope")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "not-found" });
107	  });
108	
109	  test("the owner revokes every outstanding link; members keep access, outsiders/members can't revoke", async () => {
110	    const id = await makeWorld();
111	    const first = await createInvite(asDb(), "alice", id);
112	    if (!first.ok) throw new Error("invite failed");
113	    await acceptInvite(asDb(), "bob", first.token); // bob is a member now
114	    await createInvite(asDb(), "alice", id); // a second, still-outstanding link
115	
116	    expect(await revokeInvites(asDb(), "bob", id)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "forbidden" });
117	    expect(await revokeInvites(asDb(), "mallory", id)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "not-found" });
118	
119	    expect(await revokeInvites(asDb(), "alice", id)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, revoked: 2 });
120	    expect(await resolveInvite(asDb(), first.token)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "not-found" }); // dead link
121	    expect((await getWorld(asDb(), "bob", id)).ok).toBe(true); // bob still a member
122	  });
123	});
124	
125	describe("save blobs (LWW)", () => {
126	  const blob = (text: string) => new TextEncoder().encode(text);
127	
128	  test("first upload needs a null base; later uploads need the exact stamp", async () => {
129	    const id = await makeWorld("alice", "sp-cloud");
130	
131	    const first = await putSaveBlob(asDb(), "alice", id, blob("v1"), 17, null);
132	    expect(first.ok).toBe(true);
133	    const stamp1 = first.ok ? first.updatedAt : "";
134	
135	    // A second device that never saw the blob must not clobber it.
136	    expect(await putSaveBlob(asDb(), "alice", id, blob("clobber"), 17, null)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: "conflict" });
137	    // A stale stamp must not clobber either.
138	    expect(await putSaveBlob(asDb(), "alice", id, blob("stale"), 17, "2000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z")).toMatchObject({
139	      ok: false,
140	      error: "conflict"
141	    });
142	    // The holder of the current stamp may write.
143	    const second = await putSaveBlob(asDb(), "alice", id, blob("v2"), 17, stamp1);
144	    expect(second.ok).toBe(true);
145	
146	    const fetched = await getSaveBlob(asDb(), "alice", id);
147	    expect(fetched.ok).toBe(true);
148	    if (fetched.ok) {
149	      expect(new TextDecoder().decode(fetched.blob!)).toBe("v2");
150	      expect(fetched.saveVersion).toBe(17);
151	    }
152	  });
153	
154	  test("non-members can't read or write blobs", async () => {
155	    const id = await makeWorld("alice", "sp-cloud");
156	    expect((await getSaveBlob(asDb(), "mallory", id)).ok).toBe(false);
157	    expect((await putSaveBlob(asDb(), "mallory", id, blob("x"), 17, null)).ok).toBe(false);
158	  });
159	});

164	    const minted = await mintTicket(asDb(), { id: "alice", name: "Alice", skinId: "default" }, mp, "secret-1");
165	    expect(minted.ok).toBe(true);

(Arrow/vehicle replication stays on the unit tests — `server/room.test.ts` + `NetworkSession.test.ts` — as driving them end-to-end is too environment-fragile.)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
64:E2E tests assert through `window.__monecraft` (the live engine/renderer/input handle — `net` is the session in online play; also handy in the browser console) instead of pixels; the one render check uses `renderer.renderedTriangles()`. The shared `gamePage` fixture seeds a default profile + world and enters it through the menu (reloads auto-resume the tab's world), then fails any test that logs a console error; `menu.e2e.ts` instead drives the menus from empty storage with the plain Playwright `test`.
66:Playwright boots **two** servers (`playwright.config.ts`): the Next production build with a full online stack on an in-process ephemeral Postgres (`DATABASE_URL=pglite://memory` — no Docker; `db/index.ts` applies `db/ddl.ts` at boot) and the Bun game server (`PERSISTENCE=memory`) on port 18080. The offline suites never touch either backend; `multiplayer.e2e.ts` exercises both.

455	## Multiplayer networking
456	
457	Server-side constants live in `server/room.ts` and `lib/net/protocol.ts` (not
458	`config.ts` — they shape the wire and the room budget, not gameplay balance).
459	None affect single-player, and none are save-sensitive.
460	
461	- **`ROOM_CAPACITY`** (`8`, protocol.ts) — max players per world. The v1 co-op
462	  scale the whole design assumes; raising it grows the per-tick pose/self fan-out
463	  quadratically, so re-measure with `loadSim` before nudging it.
464	- **`MAX_ROOMS`** (env, default `6`) — worlds one process hosts (memory: ~74 MB
465	  each). Joins beyond it are refused at the door, not thrashed. Tune from
466	  `/rooms` p95 tick + peak memory.
467	- **Tick rate** — `TICK_SECONDS` (`0.05` = 20 Hz, `tickDriver.ts`) is the room
468	  sim + pose-stream cadence. The whole latency budget hangs off it; not a
469	  casual dial.
470	- **Replication cadence** — `KEYFRAME_INTERVAL_TICKS` (`100` = 5 s, full mob
471	  keyframe / drift correction), mob delta frames at **10 Hz** (half tick rate)
472	  with `MOB_DEADBAND_SQ` (`0.05²` — smaller = more mob updates, more bandwidth),
473	  `DAY_INTERVAL_TICKS` (`20` = 1 s day-clock sync), `POSE_CHECKPOINT_TICKS`
474	  (`20` = 1 s replay-log pose anchors). `INTERPOLATION_DELAY_MS` (`125`,
475	  `lib/net/interpolation.ts`) is how far in the past remote entities render —
476	  larger absorbs more jitter at the cost of visible lag.
477	- **Backpressure** — `BACKPRESSURE_SOFT_BYTES` (256 KB → shed `pp`/`mp`),
478	  `BACKPRESSURE_KICK_BYTES` (1 MB) + `BACKPRESSURE_KICK_STRIKES` (~5 s sustained
479	  → `4008` kick).
480	- **Persistence** — `PERSIST_INTERVAL_TICKS` (`1200` = 60 s dirty-persist; also
481	  the crash-loss bound), idle-evict at 5 min (`roomRegistry.ts`).
482	- **Reconnect** — `RECONNECT_DELAYS_MS` (`[1,2,4,8,8] s`, protocol.ts) is the
483	  client back-off ladder; the join ticket TTL is `TICKET_TTL_SECONDS` (`60`,
484	  `tickets.ts`).
485	- **Replay log** — `COMMAND_LOG_SIZE` (env, default `4096`) bounds each room's
486	  in-memory command ring dumped by `/rooms/:id/log`.
487	- **Latency sim** — `NEXT_PUBLIC_NET_SIM_LATENCY_MS` (env, default `0`) seeds
488	  the client's artificial one-way delay; `window.__monecraft.net.setSimulatedLatency(ms)`
489	  overrides it live.
490	
491	## Save- and worldgen-sensitive tunables
492	
493	Change these only with care:
494	
```

  "new_string": "The version bump means a stale v1 client is cleanly refused at the door during a rollover. Single-player is unchanged; the boarding gate that made vehicles single-player-only is lifted.\n- **Account profiles (groundwork)**: the data layer for the \"Local Players vs Accounts\" model — a signed-in account can own up to `MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES` (**5**) server-side profiles (cross-device identities: name + skin), each owning up to `MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE` (**10**) online worlds. New `profiles` table + a `profileId` on `worlds` (drizzle migration `0001`, with a backfill that gives every existing non-guest account a default profile and hands it their worlds); owner-scoped profile CRUD with server-enforced quotas in `worldsService.ts`; `/api/profiles` routes + a browser client (`lib/online/profilesClient.ts`); and join tickets now carry the **chosen profile's** name/skin, so the in-world roster shows the profile rather than the account. Guests (anonymous) can't create profiles — online identities are account-only. Additive and offline-first: single-player and the existing online flow are unchanged until the menu is wired to profiles.\n\n### Fixed"
}

The migration also backfills: every existing non-guest\n  account gets a default profile (from its name + skin) that inherits its\n  worlds, so nothing is orphaned once the UI scopes worlds by profile.\n- MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES (5) and MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE (10) in config.ts.\n\nAdditive and offline-first: single-player and the existing online flow are\nunchanged.\n"
}

  "content": "feat(online): profile CRUD, quotas, and profile-scoped worlds/tickets\n\nThe domain rules for account profiles, in worldsService (unit-tested on PGlite):\n\n- Owner-scoped profile CRUD: listProfiles / createProfile / updateProfile /\n  deleteProfile. createProfile refuses guests (isAnonymous) — online identities\n  are account-only — and enforces MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES (a 6th is a `conflict`).\n  Deleting a profile cascades its worlds via the FK.\n- createWorld accepts an optional profileId: it must be the caller's, and the\n  profile must be under MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE (the 11th is a `conflict`). The\n  world summary now carries profileId.\n- mintTicket accepts an optional profileId and, when given, stamps the ticket's\n  name + skin from that profile (validated as the caller's) so the in-world\n  roster shows the profile, not the account. `sub` stays the account id.\n- sessionUser now surfaces isAnonymous (needed to bar guests from profiles, and\n  by the menu later).\n\nAll additive: the existing (no-profileId) create/ticket paths are unchanged.\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-hutusi-workspace-ai-david-monecraft/9d9fe046-f715-4a3d-bf2a-9bd98356707e/scratchpad/pr-body.md",
  "content": "## Local Players vs Accounts\n\nReworks the online identity model so it's coherent and the onboarding isn't backwards. Two identities:\n\n- **Local Player** (logged out) — many **local** profiles in the browser (localStorage), local single-player worlds, zero server calls. This is the offline game, unchanged.\n- **Account** (signed in) — up to **5** server-side **profiles** (cross-device identities: name + skin) that sync across devices, each owning up to **10** online worlds; hosts, invites, and joins. When signed in you see only your account's profiles/worlds; local worlds are hidden-but-preserved.\n\nLanding in phases; this PR carries the first two, with the rest to follow on this branch before merge.\n\n### ✅ Phase 1 — onboarding fix (the reported bugs)\n\n- Login / register is reachable on the **first-run** screen (previously the create-profile form took over and hid the account panel until a local profile existed).\n- A **guest** can now **Sign out** back to the login screen (previously a one-way trip).\n- `components/menu/{ProfileSelect,AccountPanel}.tsx` + unit tests; the multiplayer e2e's `Create` locator is disambiguated.\n\n### ✅ Phase 2 (backend) — account profiles, quotas, API\n\n- New `profiles` table + a `profileId` on `worlds` (drizzle migration `0001`, mirrored into `db/ddl.ts` for the PGlite paths), with a **backfill** that gives every existing non-guest account a default profile inheriting its worlds — nothing orphaned.\n- Owner-scoped profile CRUD + server-enforced quotas (`MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES=5`, `MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE=10`) in `worldsService.ts`; guests (anonymous) can't create profiles.\n- `/api/profiles` routes + `lib/online/profilesClient.ts` (total browser wrappers).\n- Join tickets now carry the **chosen profile's** name/skin, so the in-world roster shows the profile rather than the account; `sessionUser` surfaces `isAnonymous`.\n- Unit tests on PGlite for CRUD, quotas, the guest bar, delete-cascade, and the profile-stamped ticket.\n\nAdditive and offline-first: single-player and the existing online flow are unchanged, and no UI consumes profiles yet.\n\n### ⏳ Still to come on this branch\n\n- **Phase 2 (UI):** auth-aware menu — \"Play Locally\" (local profiles) vs a signed-in account showing its online profiles and their worlds; wire world creation/join through `profileId`.\n- **Phase 3:** retire the anonymous-guest layer, require sign-in to accept an invite, migrate/handle existing guest-owned online worlds, and rewrite `docs/online.md` (\"Identity: guests first\" → accounts-for-online).\n\n### Verification\n\n`bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test` (**1114 pass**), `bun run build`, and e2e (menu, multiplayer, cloudSaves, smoke) all green locally. The migration backfill runs only against real Postgres via `bun run db:migrate`; PGlite (tests, daemon-free dev) applies `db/ddl.ts`.\n"
}
```

See [[identity-rework]] (#45 → #46).\n\n**Better next time:** decide the branch name *before* opening the PR. If a rename is really needed mid-PR, expect to open a new PR rather than trusting the rename to retarget.\n"

## Status

**Phase 1 (your reported bugs) + Phase 2 backend foundation are committed, pushed, and up as a PR — all green locally.**

⚠️ One heads-up: I renamed the branch `fix/account-menu-onboarding` → `feat/local-vs-account-identity` (it's a feature now, not just a fix). The GitHub rename API **auto-closed the old PR #45** instead of retargeting it, so I opened a fresh **PR #46** with all 5 commits and left a pointer comment on #45. Recorded that gotcha in memory so I don't repeat it.

**→ PR #46: https://github.com/hutusi/monecraft/pull/46** (CI + CodeRabbit are running on it now)

Five focused commits:
1. `fix(menu)` — login reachable on first run + guest sign-out (Phase 1)
2. `feat(online)` — profiles schema, migration + backfill, quotas
3. `feat(online)` — profile CRUD, quotas, profile-scoped worlds/tickets, `sessionUser.isAnonymous`
4. `feat(online)` — `/api/profiles` routes + browser client
5. `docs` — CHANGELOG, tuning, testing

Verified: `lint`, `typecheck`, `format:check`, **`bun test` 1114 pass**, `build`, and e2e (menu/multiplayer/cloudSaves/smoke). The Phase 2 backend is deliberately **additive** — the existing online flow is untouched, so nothing broke.

## What's left on this branch (before merge)

- **Phase 2 (UI)** — the auth-aware menu: "Play Locally" (local profiles) vs a signed-in account showing its online profiles + their worlds; thread the selected profile through world-create/join. This is the visible half.
- **Phase 3** — retire the anonymous-guest layer, require sign-in to accept an invite, migrate existing guest worlds, rewrite `docs/online.md`.

One thing the Phase 2 UI forces a call on: once the menu creates/uses **account profiles**, guest-based online play stops making sense, which means reworking the guest-based `multiplayer.e2e.ts` to use a real account. So the UI step naturally pulls Phase 3's "online = account" gating in with it.

Want me to **continue with the Phase 2 UI** now (and let it pull in the account-gating), or pause here so you can review #46 and let CI/CodeRabbit weigh in first?

1	# Rework the identity model: Local Players vs Accounts
2	
3	## Context
4	
5	Multiplayer works, but the account/profile onboarding is backwards and incomplete, and the underlying identity model doesn't match how we want the game to feel:
6	
7	- **Login is blocked behind profile creation.** On first run (`components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx:27`), the zero-profiles branch renders `CreateProfileForm` full-screen and *hides* `<AccountPanel/>` (the Sign in / Register / online controls, only rendered in the profile-list view at `ProfileSelect.tsx:44`). A brand-new player literally cannot reach login/register until a local profile exists.
8	- **Guest mode is a one-way street.** In `components/menu/AccountPanel.tsx:94-97` a guest (`user.isAnonymous`) is offered *only* "Keep my worlds — create account". `signOut()` exists solely in the non-anonymous branch (`:103`), so a guest can never get back to the login screen.
9	
10	Beyond the two bugs, we're deliberately **redefining the identity model** (this reverses the current "guests first" design in `docs/online.md`):
11	
12	- **Local Player** (logged out): keeps many **local profiles** in the browser (localStorage, exactly as today), owns local single-player worlds, makes **zero** server calls. Cannot create, invite to, *or join* anything online. Entry point is **"Play Locally"**; the list is **"Local Profiles"**.
13	- **Account** (logged in): owns up to **5 online profiles** stored server-side that sync across devices; each profile owns up to **10 online worlds**; can host, invite, and join. When logged in you see **only** your account's online profiles and their worlds — existing local worlds are **hidden but preserved** (never deleted, never auto-uploaded), and reappear when you play as a Local Player again.
14	
15	Guests-as-anonymous-server-users go away entirely: online == you have an account.
16	
17	**Sequencing:** ship as three landable phases. Phase 1 fixes the two reported bugs on its own and is low-risk. Phases 2–3 build the new model and retire the guest layer.
18	
19	---
20	
21	## Target model (agreed rules)
22	
23	| | Local Player (logged out) | Account (logged in) |
24	|---|---|---|
25	| Profiles | many, local (localStorage) | up to **5**, server-side, synced |
26	| Worlds | local single-player only | online only, up to **10 per profile** |
27	| Online create / invite / join | ❌ none | ✅ |
28	| Storage | browser only, no network | server (`worlds` rows + `saveBlob`) |
29	| On login | local worlds **hidden but preserved** | shows only account's online profiles/worlds |
30	
31	---
32	
33	## Phase 1 — Entry-UX fix (the two reported bugs) · independently shippable
34	
35	Small, low-risk, no data-model change. Keeps today's backend semantics.
36	
37	1. **Login reachable on first run** — `components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx`. Render `<AccountPanel/>` in the first-run (`firstRun`) branch too, above the create-profile form, so Sign in / Register are always reachable. Safe for offline-first: `AccountPanel`'s effect only calls the server when `onlineUsed()` is true (`AccountPanel.tsx:24`), so a brand-new offline player still makes no auth request.
38	2. **Guest can return to login** — `components/menu/AccountPanel.tsx:94-97`. In the `user.isAnonymous` branch add a **"Sign out"** button mirroring the existing non-anonymous handler (`:102-105`: `await authClient().signOut(); await refresh();`), which drops back to the "Offline" state where "Sign in" / "Play online as guest" already live.
39	3. **Tests** — update `components/menu/ProfileSelect.test.tsx` (the "no profiles → no cancel" test now also expects the account panel present) and `components/menu/AccountPanel.test.tsx` (add: a guest can sign out and returns to "Offline").
40	4. **Docs/changelog** — `CHANGELOG.md` entry; note the fix in `docs/online.md`.
41	
42	---
43	
44	## Phase 2 — Local vs Account identity, server profiles, quotas
45	
46	The core feature. Introduces a server-side profile entity and makes the menu auth-aware.
47	
48	### Server: profiles table + per-profile worlds
49	- **Schema** — add a `profiles` table to `db/schema.ts` and `db/ddl.ts` (mirror `user`/`worlds` style): `{ id, ownerId → user.id (cascade), name, skinId, createdAt }`. Add a nullable `profileId → profiles.id` column to the `worlds` table (the account still owns via `ownerId`; `profileId` says *which* profile within the account). Add a drizzle migration under `db/migrations`, and a data backfill: for each existing account create one default profile (from the account's `name` + `skinId`) and stamp its existing worlds with that `profileId`.
50	- **Service + quotas** — in `lib/online/worldsService.ts`: add profile CRUD (`listProfiles`/`createProfile`/`renameProfile`/`setProfileSkin`/`deleteProfile`, scoped to `ownerId`) and enforce quotas at write time — reject the 6th profile per account and the 11th world per profile (count-before-insert, returning the existing `failure`/`STATUS` shape). Put the limits in `config.ts` (`MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES = 5`, `MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE = 10`) so they're documented in `docs/tuning.md`.
51	- **Routes** — add `app/api/profiles/route.ts` (GET list / POST create) and `app/api/profiles/[id]/route.ts` (PATCH / DELETE), thin adapters over the service using the existing `sessionUser` + `unauthorized()` pattern from `lib/online/http.ts`.
52	- **Gate online to accounts** — surface `isAnonymous` in `sessionUser` (`lib/online/http.ts` currently drops it) and reject anonymous users in `createWorld`, `createInvite`, and `mintTicket` (`worldsService.ts`). One choke point; routes need no change.
53	- **Ticket identity = the profile** — `mintTicket` should stamp `name`/`skinId` from the *selected profile*, not the account. The ticket route (`app/api/worlds/[id]/ticket/route.ts`) takes a `profileId`, validated to belong to the caller. (Decide during impl whether the game server's per-player save slice keys on account `sub` or on `profileId` — see Open decisions.)
54	
55	### Client
56	- **Online profiles wrapper** — new `lib/online/profilesClient.ts` mirroring `lib/online/onlineClient.ts` (total functions returning `null`/`false`): `listOnlineProfiles`, `createOnlineProfile`, `renameOnlineProfile`, `deleteOnlineProfile`.
57	- **Auth-aware ProfileSelect** — `components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx` becomes the hub. Logged out → local profiles from `lib/game/profiles.ts` under a **"Play Locally"** heading (unchanged behavior). Logged in → fetch and list **online profiles**; local worlds are simply not shown. The account panel gains a clear **Sign out** → returns to local mode. Rename UI copy: guest/"Play online as guest" → "Play Locally" / "Sign in / Register".
58	- **World lists** — `components/menu/WorldSelect.tsx` already splits local / Cloud Saves / Online Worlds. In account mode it shows the selected online profile's worlds (server `worlds` filtered by `profileId`), reusing `createOnlineWorld`/`listOnlineWorlds`/invite helpers. The existing `sp-cloud` cloud-save storage layer (`lib/game/cloudSaves.ts`, `putSaveBlob`/`getSaveBlob`) is the storage for account worlds — no new blob path needed.
59	- **GameShell wiring** — `components/GameShell.tsx` threads the selected profile (local `Profile` or online profile) into play, and passes `profileId` to `playOnline` → ticket request.
60	
61	### Tests
62	- `lib/online/worldsService.test.ts` (or existing service tests): quota enforcement (6th profile, 11th world), anonymous rejection on create/invite/ticket, ticket carries profile identity.
63	- Component tests for the auth-aware `ProfileSelect` (local vs account mode) and the new profiles client.
64	- Update the coverage map in `docs/testing.md`.
65	
66	---
67	
68	## Phase 3 — Retire the anonymous-guest layer, rewire invites, migrate, docs
69	
70	1. **Remove anonymous auth** — delete the `anonymous()` plugin + `onLinkAccount` re-parenting (`lib/auth/server.ts:41-68`), `anonymousClient()` and the `ensureSignedIn` anonymous path (`lib/auth/client.ts:22,58-65`), the `isAnonymous` column (`db/schema.ts:32`, `db/ddl.ts`), and the guest branches in `AccountPanel.tsx`. The game server and ticket format need no change (they never referenced anonymity).
71	2. **Invites require sign-in** — `app/join/[token]/page.tsx:26` currently calls `ensureSignedIn()` (mints a guest). Change unauthenticated visitors to a real Sign in / Register step (the world name still previews via the no-auth `GET /api/invite/:token`), then accept the invite, then **pick which profile** joins.
72	3. **Migration of existing deployed data** — accounts and their worlds are safe (backfilled in Phase 2). Existing **guest-owned** online worlds/memberships can't survive removing anonymous users; default plan: drop them via the migration (or leave orphaned) and call it out in release notes. Existing **local** profiles/worlds are untouched.
73	4. **Docs** — rewrite `docs/online.md` "Identity: guests first" → "accounts for online, Local Players for offline"; update `docs/save-format.md` (server profiles, per-profile worlds), `docs/protocol.md` (ticket carries profile identity), `docs/reference.md`/`docs/manual.md` (quotas, Play Locally), and `CHANGELOG.md`. Remove/replace `lib/auth/auth.test.ts` (the onLinkAccount test) and the guest-path e2e in `e2e/multiplayer.e2e.ts`.
74	
75	---
76	
77	## Branching, commits & PRs
78	
79	- **Phase 1** (reported bugs) ships **first, on its own** — a small focused PR (or straight to `main`, since it's a pure bug fix with no model change), so live users get the fix without waiting on the redesign.
80	- **Phases 2 + 3** land **together** on one branch `feat/local-vs-account-identity` off `main`, as **one PR** — they must ship as a unit so production never sees the half-migrated state (anonymous guests still minting while account-profiles are already live). Within the PR, one focused Conventional-Commit per slice: schema+migration → service+quotas → profile routes → auth-aware client/UI → remove anonymous layer → invite rewire → docs/tests. Keep `bun run lint` green at each commit (bisectable).
81	- Why-focused commit bodies; **no** `Co-Authored-By` and **no** AI-attribution lines in the PR description. Open against `hutusi/monecraft` `main`. Pushing / opening PRs is user-authorized — not done until you ask.
82	
83	## Verification
84	
85	- **Per phase:** `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build` (the CI `verify` gate); `bun run test:e2e` for Phases touching the React shell / renderer (all three).
86	- **Phase 1 manual:** fresh browser (clear localStorage) → first screen shows both Sign in / Register **and** the create-profile form (login no longer blocked). Enter guest → a "Sign out" returns to the login screen.
87	- **Phase 2 manual (zero-service online):** run with `DATABASE_URL=pglite://memory` + `PERSISTENCE=memory` (`docs/online.md` "No Docker at all"): sign in → see online profiles (not local worlds); create a 6th profile → rejected; create an 11th world under a profile → rejected; local worlds reappear after Sign out.
88	- **Phase 3 manual:** an invite link while logged out routes to Sign in / Register (no silent guest); after signing in and picking a profile, the world joins. Confirm no `signIn.anonymous` path remains (grep).
89	
90	## Open decisions / risks (resolve during implementation)
91	
92	- **Save-slice keying for shared MP worlds:** account (`sub`) vs `profileId`. Simplest MVP keys by account; profile-level identity is the "purer" model but touches save v-format + the game server. Decide in Phase 2.
93	- **Existing guest-owned online worlds** on the live deployment: confirm "drop with release-note warning" is acceptable, or add a one-time upgrade prompt before Phase 3 ships.
94	- **Offline-first invariant** stays intact: no server call for a logged-out Local Player (keep the `onlineUsed()` gate before any session fetch).
95	
```

===== INLINE REVIEW COMMENTS (#45) =====
### coderabbitai[bot] — components/menu/AccountPanel.tsx:31
_🩺 Stability & Availability_ | _🟠 Major_ | _⚡ Quick win_

**`signOut` lacks error handling and busy-state guarding, unlike other async handlers in this file.**

`submit` and the "Play online as guest" handler both wrap their async calls in `try/catch` with `busy`/`error` state management. `signOut` does neither: if `authClient().signOut()` throws (e.g., network failure), the promise rejects unhandled, `refresh()` never runs, and the UI is left in a stale/inconsistent state with no feedback to the user. It's also not disabled while pending, so rapid double-clicks can fire concurrent sign-out calls.

<details>
<summary>🐛 Proposed fix to align signOut with the existing error-handling pattern</summary>

```diff
   const refresh = async () => setUser(await currentUser());
   const signOut = async () => {
-    await authClient().signOut();
-    await refresh();
+    setBusy(true);
+    setError(null);
+    try {
+      await authClient().signOut();
+      await refresh();
+    } catch {
+      setError("Couldn't reach the server — check your connection and try again.");
+    } finally {
+      setBusy(false);
+    }
   };
```
</details>





Also applies to: 99-113

in AccountPanel should follow the same async pattern as submit and the guest
action: add busy/error state guarding, wrap authClient().signOut() and refresh()
in try/catch, and clear busy in a finally block. Update the sign-out UI control
to be disabled while the action is pending so repeated clicks cannot trigger
concurrent sign-outs, and surface any failure through the existing error state
handling.
```

</details>

## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Rework the identity model: Local Players vs Accounts

## Context

Multiplayer works, but the account/profile onboarding is backwards and incomplete, and the underlying identity model doesn't match how we want the game to feel:

- **Login is blocked behind profile creation.** On first run (`components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx:27`), the zero-profiles branch renders `CreateProfileForm` full-screen and *hides* `<AccountPanel/>` (the Sign in / Register / online controls, only rendered in the profile-list view at `ProfileSelect.tsx:44`). A brand-new player literally cannot reach login/register until a local profile exists.
- **Guest mode is a one-way street.** In `components/menu/AccountPanel.tsx:94-97` a guest (`user.isAnonymous`) is offered *only* "Keep my worlds — create account". `signOut()` exists solely in the non-anonymous branch (`:103`), so a guest can never get back to the login screen.

Beyond the two bugs, we're deliberately **redefining the identity model** (this reverses the current "guests first" design in `docs/online.md`):

- **Local Player** (logged out): keeps many **local profiles** in the browser (localStorage, exactly as today), owns local single-player worlds, makes **zero** server calls. Cannot create, invite to, *or join* anything online. Entry point is **"Play Locally"**; the list is **"Local Profiles"**.
- **Account** (logged in): owns up to **5 online profiles** stored server-side that sync across devices; each profile owns up to **10 online worlds**; can host, invite, and join. When logged in you see **only** your account's online profiles and their worlds — existing local worlds are **hidden but preserved** (never deleted, never auto-uploaded), and reappear when you play as a Local Player again.

Guests-as-anonymous-server-users go away entirely: online == you have an account.

**Sequencing:** ship as three landable phases. Phase 1 fixes the two reported bugs on its own and is low-risk. Phases 2–3 build the new model and retire the guest layer.

---

## Status & immediate next step

Everything is on one branch `feat/local-vs-account-identity` → **PR #46** (the first push was `fix/account-menu-onboarding`/PR #45; renaming the branch auto-closed #45, so #46 supersedes it).

- **DONE (committed + pushed):** Phase 1 (the two reported bugs) + the **Phase 2 backend** — `profiles` table + `worlds.profileId` (migration `0001` + backfill, mirrored in `db/ddl.ts`), quotas in `config.ts`, owner-scoped profile CRUD + guest-bar + quotas in `worldsService.ts`, `sessionUser.isAnonymous`, optional `profileId` on `createWorld`/`mintTicket` (ticket stamps the profile's name/skin), `/api/profiles` routes + `lib/online/profilesClient.ts`, unit tests. All additive/green (1114 tests). CI `verify` + Vercel passed on #45.
- **REMAINING:** the CodeRabbit review fix below (immediate), then Phase 2 **UI** (auth-aware menu) and Phase 3 (retire the guest layer). See those sections.

### Immediate — apply the PR #45 CodeRabbit review fix

CodeRabbit left exactly **one** actionable finding (Major / quick win): the Phase 1 `signOut` helper in `components/menu/AccountPanel.tsx` (`:28-31`) calls `authClient().signOut()` + `refresh()` with **no error handling or busy-guard**, unlike every other async handler in the file (`submit`, the guest action). Fix:

1. Wrap `signOut` in the file's established pattern: `setBusy(true)` / `setError(null)` → `try { await authClient().signOut(); await refresh(); } catch { setError("Couldn't reach the server — check your connection and try again."); } finally { setBusy(false); }`.
2. Add `disabled={busy}` to **both** Sign out buttons (`:105`, `:110`) and the guest "Keep my worlds — create account" (`:100`) so a double-click can't fire concurrent sign-outs.
3. Render `{error && <div className="account-error">{error}</div>}` in the signed-in/guest (status) branch too — today it renders only in the logged-out branch (`:140`), so a sign-out failure would set state nobody can see.
4. **Test:** extend `components/menu/AccountPanel.test.tsx` — make the fake `authClient().signOut()` reject once and assert the error surfaces and the panel stays usable.

No CHANGELOG change (refinement of the same Unreleased Phase 1 fix). Verify with `bun test components/menu/AccountPanel.test.tsx`, then the full gate (`lint`/`typecheck`/`format:check`/`bun test`/`build`) before committing to `feat/local-vs-account-identity` and pushing to #46.

---

## Target model (agreed rules)

| | Local Player (logged out) | Account (logged in) |
|---|---|---|
| Profiles | many, local (localStorage) | up to **5**, server-side, synced |
| Worlds | local single-player only | online only, up to **10 per profile** |
| Online create / invite / join | ❌ none | ✅ |
| Storage | browser only, no network | server (`worlds` rows + `saveBlob`) |
| On login | local worlds **hidden but preserved** | shows only account's online profiles/worlds |

---

## Phase 1 — Entry-UX fix (the two reported bugs) · independently shippable

Small, low-risk, no data-model change. Keeps today's backend semantics.

1. **Login reachable on first run** — `components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx`. Render `<AccountPanel/>` in the first-run (`firstRun`) branch too, above the create-profile form, so Sign in / Register are always reachable. Safe for offline-first: `AccountPanel`'s effect only calls the server when `onlineUsed()` is true (`AccountPanel.tsx:24`), so a brand-new offline player still makes no auth request.
2. **Guest can return to login** — `components/menu/AccountPanel.tsx:94-97`. In the `user.isAnonymous` branch add a **"Sign out"** button mirroring the existing non-anonymous handler (`:102-105`: `await authClient().signOut(); await refresh();`), which drops back to the "Offline" state where "Sign in" / "Play online as guest" already live.
3. **Tests** — update `components/menu/ProfileSelect.test.tsx` (the "no profiles → no cancel" test now also expects the account panel present) and `components/menu/AccountPanel.test.tsx` (add: a guest can sign out and returns to "Offline").
4. **Docs/changelog** — `CHANGELOG.md` entry; note the fix in `docs/online.md`.

---

## Phase 2 — Local vs Account identity, server profiles, quotas

The core feature. Introduces a server-side profile entity and makes the menu auth-aware. **The "Server", "Client → Online profiles wrapper", and "Tests → service" parts below are DONE and pushed (see Status); the remaining Phase 2 work is the auth-aware UI (ProfileSelect/WorldSelect/GameShell) + its component tests.**

### Server: profiles table + per-profile worlds
- **Schema** — add a `profiles` table to `db/schema.ts` and `db/ddl.ts` (mirror `user`/`worlds` style): `{ id, ownerId → user.id (cascade), name, skinId, createdAt }`. Add a nullable `profileId → profiles.id` column to the `worlds` table (the account still owns via `ownerId`; `profileId` says *which* profile within the account). Add a drizzle migration under `db/migrations`, and a data backfill: for each existing account create one default profile (from the account's `name` + `skinId`) and stamp its existing worlds with that `profileId`.
- **Service + quotas** — in `lib/online/worldsService.ts`: add profile CRUD (`listProfiles`/`createProfile`/`renameProfile`/`setProfileSkin`/`deleteProfile`, scoped to `ownerId`) and enforce quotas at write time — reject the 6th profile per account and the 11th world per profile (count-before-insert, returning the existing `failure`/`STATUS` shape). Put the limits in `config.ts` (`MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES = 5`, `MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE = 10`) so they're documented in `docs/tuning.md`.
- **Routes** — add `app/api/profiles/route.ts` (GET list / POST create) and `app/api/profiles/[id]/route.ts` (PATCH / DELETE), thin adapters over the service using the existing `sessionUser` + `unauthorized()` pattern from `lib/online/http.ts`.
- **Gate online to accounts** — surface `isAnonymous` in `sessionUser` (`lib/online/http.ts` currently drops it) and reject anonymous users in `createWorld`, `createInvite`, and `mintTicket` (`worldsService.ts`). One choke point; routes need no change.
- **Ticket identity = the profile** — `mintTicket` should stamp `name`/`skinId` from the *selected profile*, not the account. The ticket route (`app/api/worlds/[id]/ticket/route.ts`) takes a `profileId`, validated to belong to the caller. (Decide during impl whether the game server's per-player save slice keys on account `sub` or on `profileId` — see Open decisions.)

### Client
- **Online profiles wrapper** — new `lib/online/profilesClient.ts` mirroring `lib/online/onlineClient.ts` (total functions returning `null`/`false`): `listOnlineProfiles`, `createOnlineProfile`, `renameOnlineProfile`, `deleteOnlineProfile`.
- **Auth-aware ProfileSelect** — `components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx` becomes the hub. Logged out → local profiles from `lib/game/profiles.ts` under a **"Play Locally"** heading (unchanged behavior). Logged in → fetch and list **online profiles**; local worlds are simply not shown. The account panel gains a clear **Sign out** → returns to local mode. Rename UI copy: guest/"Play online as guest" → "Play Locally" / "Sign in / Register".
- **World lists** — `components/menu/WorldSelect.tsx` already splits local / Cloud Saves / Online Worlds. In account mode it shows the selected online profile's worlds (server `worlds` filtered by `profileId`), reusing `createOnlineWorld`/`listOnlineWorlds`/invite helpers. The existing `sp-cloud` cloud-save storage layer (`lib/game/cloudSaves.ts`, `putSaveBlob`/`getSaveBlob`) is the storage for account worlds — no new blob path needed.
- **GameShell wiring** — `components/GameShell.tsx` threads the selected profile (local `Profile` or online profile) into play, and passes `profileId` to `playOnline` → ticket request.

### Tests
- `lib/online/worldsService.test.ts` (or existing service tests): quota enforcement (6th profile, 11th world), anonymous rejection on create/invite/ticket, ticket carries profile identity.
- Component tests for the auth-aware `ProfileSelect` (local vs account mode) and the new profiles client.
- Update the coverage map in `docs/testing.md`.

---

## Phase 3 — Retire the anonymous-guest layer, rewire invites, migrate, docs

1. **Remove anonymous auth** — delete the `anonymous()` plugin + `onLinkAccount` re-parenting (`lib/auth/server.ts:41-68`), `anonymousClient()` and the `ensureSignedIn` anonymous path (`lib/auth/client.ts:22,58-65`), the `isAnonymous` column (`db/schema.ts:32`, `db/ddl.ts`), and the guest branches in `AccountPanel.tsx`. The game server and ticket format need no change (they never referenced anonymity).
2. **Invites require sign-in** — `app/join/[token]/page.tsx:26` currently calls `ensureSignedIn()` (mints a guest). Change unauthenticated visitors to a real Sign in / Register step (the world name still previews via the no-auth `GET /api/invite/:token`), then accept the invite, then **pick which profile** joins.
3. **Migration of existing deployed data** — accounts and their worlds are safe (backfilled in Phase 2). Existing **guest-owned** online worlds/memberships can't survive removing anonymous users; default plan: drop them via the migration (or leave orphaned) and call it out in release notes. Existing **local** profiles/worlds are untouched.
4. **Docs** — rewrite `docs/online.md` "Identity: guests first" → "accounts for online, Local Players for offline"; update `docs/save-format.md` (server profiles, per-profile worlds), `docs/protocol.md` (ticket carries profile identity), `docs/reference.md`/`docs/manual.md` (quotas, Play Locally), and `CHANGELOG.md`. Remove/replace `lib/auth/auth.test.ts` (the onLinkAccount test) and the guest-path e2e in `e2e/multiplayer.e2e.ts`.

---

## Branching, commits & PRs

- **Phase 1** (reported bugs) ships **first, on its own** — a small focused PR (or straight to `main`, since it's a pure bug fix with no model change), so live users get the fix without waiting on the redesign.
- **Phases 2 + 3** land **together** on one branch `feat/local-vs-account-identity` off `main`, as **one PR** — they must ship as a unit so production never sees the half-migrated state (anonymous guests still minting while account-profiles are already live). Within the PR, one focused Conventional-Commit per slice: schema+migration → service+quotas → profile routes → auth-aware client/UI → remove anonymous layer → invite rewire → docs/tests. Keep `bun run lint` green at each commit (bisectable).
- Why-focused commit bodies; **no** `Co-Authored-By` and **no** AI-attribution lines in the PR description. Open against `hutusi/monecraft` `main`. Pushing / opening PRs is user-authorized — not done until you ask.

## Verification

- **Per phase:** `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build` (the CI `verify` gate); `bun run test:e2e` for Phases touching the React shell / renderer (all three).
- **Phase 1 manual:** fresh browser (clear localStorage) → first screen shows both Sign in / Register **and** the create-profile form (login no longer blocked). Enter guest → a "Sign out" returns to the login screen.
- **Phase 2 manual (zero-service online):** run with `DATABASE_URL=pglite://memory` + `PERSISTENCE=memory` (`docs/online.md` "No Docker at all"): sign in → see online profiles (not local worlds); create a 6th profile → rejected; create an 11th world under a profile → rejected; local worlds reappear after Sign out.
- **Phase 3 manual:** an invite link while logged out routes to Sign in / Register (no silent guest); after signing in and picking a profile, the world joins. Confirm no `signIn.anonymous` path remains (grep).

## Open decisions / risks (resolve during implementation)

- **Save-slice keying for shared MP worlds:** account (`sub`) vs `profileId`. Simplest MVP keys by account; profile-level identity is the "purer" model but touches save v-format + the game server. Decide in Phase 2.
- **Existing guest-owned online worlds** on the live deployment: confirm "drop with release-note warning" is acceptable, or add a one-time upgrade prompt before Phase 3 ships.
- **Offline-first invariant** stays intact: no server call for a logged-out Local Player (keep the `onlineUsed()` gate before any session fetch).

7	type CreateProfileFormProps = {

13	/** The new-profile form: a name and a skin picker, reusing the pause-menu swatch grid. */
14	export default function CreateProfileForm({ onCreate, onCancel }: CreateProfileFormProps) {

18	  return (

31	          value={name}

47	              aria-pressed={preset.id === skinId}
48	              aria-label={`${preset.label} skin`}
49	              onClick={() => setSkinId(preset.id)}
50	            >
51	              <PixelImg src={skinPortraitUrl(preset.id)} alt="" size={32} aria-hidden />
52	              <span>{preset.label}</span>
53	            </button>
54	          ))}
55	        </div>
56	      </div>
57	      <div className="menu-form-actions">
58	        <button type="submit" className="mc-button">
59	          Create
60	        </button>
61	        {onCancel ? (
62	          <button type="button" className="mc-button" onClick={onCancel}>
63	            Cancel
64	          </button>
65	        ) : null}
66	      </div>
67	    </form>
68	  );
69	}

1	import { useState } from "react";
2	import PixelImg from "@/components/game/PixelImg";
3	import AccountPanel from "@/components/menu/AccountPanel";
4	import CreateProfileForm from "@/components/menu/CreateProfileForm";
5	import MenuScreen from "@/components/menu/MenuScreen";
6	import { createProfile, deleteProfile, MAX_PROFILE_NAME, readProfiles, renameProfile } from "@/lib/game/profiles";
7	import { skinPortraitUrl } from "@/lib/ui/sprites";
8	import { deleteWorldsForProfile, worldsForProfile } from "@/lib/game/worlds";
9	
10	type ProfileSelectProps = {
11	  /** Enter a profile: select it and show its worlds. */
12	  onPlay: (profileId: string) => void;
13	};
14	
15	/** The top menu: pick a player profile, or create / rename / delete one. */
16	export default function ProfileSelect({ onPlay }: ProfileSelectProps) {
17	  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
18	  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
19	  const [editName, setEditName] = useState("");
20	  const [confirmingDeleteId, setConfirmingDeleteId] = useState<string | null>(null);
21	
22	  const { profiles } = readProfiles();
23	  // With no profiles (first run, or the last one was just deleted) there is
24	  // nothing to select, so open straight into the create form with no Cancel.
25	  const firstRun = profiles.length === 0;
26	
27	  if (creating || firstRun) {
28	    return (
29	      <MenuScreen title={firstRun ? "Create Your Profile" : "New Profile"}>
30	        {/* First run has no profile list to host the account controls, so surface
31	            them here too — otherwise sign in / register is unreachable until a
32	            local profile exists. (The list view renders its own panel below.) */}
33	        {firstRun && <AccountPanel />}
34	        <CreateProfileForm
35	          onCreate={(name, skinId) => {
36	            const profile = createProfile(name, skinId);
37	            setCreating(false);
38	            onPlay(profile.id); // straight into the new profile's (empty) world list
39	          }}
40	          onCancel={firstRun ? undefined : () => setCreating(false)}
41	        />
42	      </MenuScreen>
43	    );
44	  }
45	
46	  return (
47	    <MenuScreen title="Select Profile">
48	      <AccountPanel />
49	      <ul className="menu-list">
50	        {profiles.map((profile) => (
51	          <li key={profile.id} className="menu-card">
52	            {editingId === profile.id ? (
53	              <form
54	                className="menu-rename"
55	                onSubmit={(event) => {
56	                  event.preventDefault();
57	                  renameProfile(profile.id, editName);
58	                  setEditingId(null); // re-render re-reads the manifest
59	                }}
60	              >
61	                <input
62	                  className="menu-input"
63	                  value={editName}
64	                  maxLength={MAX_PROFILE_NAME}
65	                  autoFocus
66	                  aria-label="Rename profile"
67	                  onChange={(event) => setEditName(event.target.value)}
68	                />
69	                <button type="submit" className="mc-button">
70	                  Save
71	                </button>
72	                <button type="button" className="mc-button" onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}>
73	                  Cancel
74	                </button>
75	              </form>
76	            ) : confirmingDeleteId === profile.id ? (
77	              <div className="menu-confirm">
78	                <span>Delete “{profile.name}” and all its worlds?</span>
79	                <div className="menu-confirm-actions">
80	                  <button
81	                    className="mc-button danger"
82	                    onClick={() => {
83	                      deleteWorldsForProfile(profile.id);
84	                      deleteProfile(profile.id);
85	                      setConfirmingDeleteId(null); // re-render re-reads the manifest
86	                    }}
87	                  >
88	                    Delete
89	                  </button>
90	                  <button className="mc-button" onClick={() => setConfirmingDeleteId(null)}>
91	                    Cancel
92	                  </button>
93	                </div>
94	              </div>
95	            ) : (
96	              <>
97	                <button className="menu-card-play" data-testid={`profile-${profile.id}`} onClick={() => onPlay(profile.id)}>
98	                  <PixelImg src={skinPortraitUrl(profile.skinId)} alt="" size={40} aria-hidden />
99	                  <span className="menu-card-name">{profile.name}</span>
100	                  <span className="menu-card-sub">{worldsForProfile(profile.id).length} worlds</span>
101	                </button>
102	                <div className="menu-card-actions">
103	                  <button
104	                    className="mc-button"
105	                    onClick={() => {
106	                      setEditName(profile.name);
107	                      setEditingId(profile.id);
108	                    }}
109	                  >
110	                    Rename
111	                  </button>
112	                  <button className="mc-button" onClick={() => setConfirmingDeleteId(profile.id)}>
113	                    Delete
114	                  </button>
115	                </div>
116	              </>
117	            )}
118	          </li>
119	        ))}
120	      </ul>
121	      <button className="mc-button menu-primary" data-testid="new-profile" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>
122	        New Profile
123	      </button>
124	    </MenuScreen>
125	  );
126	}
127	
```

> AGENT

Now I have all the menu pieces. This Phase 2 UI is the big rework: an auth-aware menu where a signed-in account browses its **online profiles → online worlds**, while logged-out stays the local flow (guest removal is Phase 3, so this stays additive — the guest e2e keeps passing). I'll build it in slices, committing each locally.

Let me start with the account-mode leaf component — the online-worlds list for a selected profile:

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport CreateWorldForm from \"@/components/menu/CreateWorldForm\";\nimport MenuScreen from \"@/components/menu/MenuScreen\";\nimport { GAME_MODE_PRESETS, type GameMode } from \"@/lib/game/gameModes\";\nimport { DIFFICULTY_PRESETS, type Difficulty } from \"@/lib/game/difficulties\";\nimport { MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE } from \"@/lib/game/config\";\nimport { MAX_WORLD_NAME, resolveSeed, WORLD_TYPE_PRESETS } from \"@/lib/game/worlds\";\nimport type { WorldType } from \"@/lib/world\";\nimport { createOnlineWorld, listOnlineWorlds, createInviteLink, revokeInviteLinks, type OnlineWorld } from \"@/lib/online/onlineClient\";\nimport type { OnlineProfile } from \"@/lib/online/profilesClient\";\n\n/**\n * An account profile's online worlds: the account-mode counterpart to\n * WorldSelect. Lists only this profile's server-hosted (mp) worlds, creates new\n * ones (capped at MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE), and copies/revokes invite links. All\n * worlds here live on the server and belong to `profile` via `world.profileId`.\n */\n\nfunction worldTypeLabel(id: WorldType): string {\n  return WORLD_TYPE_PRESETS.find((preset) => preset.id === id)?.label ?? id;\n}\nfunction gameModeLabel(id: GameMode): string {\n  return GAME_MODE_PRESETS.find((preset) => preset.id === id)?.label ?? id;\n}\nfunction difficultyLabel(id: Difficulty): string {\n  return DIFFICULTY_PRESETS.find((preset) => preset.id === id)?.label ?? id;\n}\n\ntype OnlineWorldSelectProps = {\n  profile: OnlineProfile;\n  /** Join a server-hosted world as this profile. */\n  onPlay: (world: OnlineWorld) => void;\n  /** Back to the account's profile list. */\n  onBack: () => void;\n};\n\nexport default function OnlineWorldSelect({ profile, onPlay, onBack }: OnlineWorldSelectProps) {\n  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);\n  const [worlds, setWorlds] = useState<OnlineWorld[] | null>(null);\n  const [createError, setCreateError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [inviteCopied, setInviteCopied] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [inviteError, setInviteError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [invitesRevoked, setInvitesRevoked] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Only this profile's server rooms (the account may own others under a\n  // different profile); listOnlineWorlds returns everything the account plays.\n  const refresh = useCallback(() => {\n    void listOnlineWorlds().then((all) => setWorlds((all ?? []).filter((world) => world.kind === \"mp\" && world.profileId === profile.id)));\n  }, [profile.id]);\n  useEffect(() => refresh(), [refresh]);\n\n  const atCap = (worlds?.length ?? 0) >= MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE;\n\n  if (creating) {\n    return (\n      <MenuScreen title={`${profile.name} — New Online World`}>\n        {createError && <p className=\"account-error\">{createError}</p>}\n        <CreateWorldForm\n          onCreate={(name, seed, worldType, gameMode, difficulty, hardcore) => {\n            setCreateError(null);\n            void createOnlineWorld({ name, seed: resolveSeed(seed), worldType, gameMode, difficulty, hardcore, profileId: profile.id }).then((world) => {\n              if (world) {\n                setCreating(false);\n                onPlay(world);\n              } else {\n                setCreateError(\"Couldn't create the world — are you online, and under your world limit?\");\n              }\n            });\n          }}\n          onCancel={() => setCreating(false)}\n        />\n      </MenuScreen>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <MenuScreen title={`${profile.name} — Online Worlds`}>\n      {worlds === null ? (\n        <p className=\"menu-empty\">Loading…</p>\n      ) : worlds.length === 0 ? (\n        <p className=\"menu-empty\">No online worlds yet — create one and share the invite link.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <ul className=\"menu-list\">\n          {worlds.map((world) => (\n            <li key={world.id} className=\"menu-card\">\n              <button className=\"menu-card-play\" data-testid={`online-world-${world.id}`} onClick={() => onPlay(world)}>\n                <span className=\"menu-card-name\">{world.name}</span>\n                <span className=\"menu-card-sub\">\n                  {world.role === \"owner\" ? \"Your world\" : \"Joined\"} ·{world.hardcore ? \" Hardcore ·\" : \"\"}\n                  {world.gameMode !== \"survival\" ? ` ${gameModeLabel(world.gameMode)} ·` : \"\"}\n                  {!world.hardcore && world.difficulty !== \"normal\" ? ` ${difficultyLabel(world.difficulty)} ·` : \"\"}\n                  {world.worldType !== \"default\" ? ` ${worldTypeLabel(world.worldType)} ·` : \"\"} Seed {world.seed}\n                </span>\n              </button>\n              {world.role === \"owner\" && (\n                <div className=\"menu-card-actions\">\n                  <button\n                    className=\"mc-button\"\n                    onClick={() => {\n                      setInvitesRevoked(null);\n                      setInviteError(null);\n                      setInviteCopied(null);\n                      void createInviteLink(world.id).then((link) => {\n                        const clipboard = navigator.clipboard;\n                        if (!link || !clipboard) return void setInviteError(world.id);\n                        void clipboard\n                          .writeText(link)\n                          .then(() => setInviteCopied(world.id))\n                          .catch(() => setInviteError(world.id));\n                      });\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    {inviteError === world.id ? \"Copy failed\" : inviteCopied === world.id ? \"Link copied!\" : \"Copy invite\"}\n                  </button>\n                  <button\n                    className=\"mc-button\"\n                    title=\"Invalidate every invite link you've shared for this world\"\n                    onClick={() => {\n                      setInviteCopied(null);\n                      setInviteError(null);\n                      void revokeInviteLinks(world.id).then((count) => {\n                        if (count !== null) setInvitesRevoked(world.id);\n                      });\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    {invitesRevoked === world.id ? \"Links revoked\" : \"Revoke links\"}\n                  </button>\n                </div>\n              )}\n            </li>\n          ))}\n        </ul>\n      )}\n      <div className=\"menu-bottom-row\">\n        <button className=\"mc-button\" data-testid=\"back-to-profiles\" onClick={onBack}>\n          Back\n        </button>\n        <button\n          className=\"mc-button menu-primary\"\n          data-testid=\"new-online-world\"\n          disabled={atCap}\n          title={atCap ? `Profile world limit reached (${MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE})` : undefined}\n          onClick={() => setCreating(true)}\n        >\n          {atCap ? `World limit reached (${MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE})` : \"New Online World\"}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </MenuScreen>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport PixelImg from \"@/components/game/PixelImg\";\nimport CreateProfileForm from \"@/components/menu/CreateProfileForm\";\nimport MenuScreen from \"@/components/menu/MenuScreen\";\nimport { MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES } from \"@/lib/game/config\";\nimport { DEFAULT_SKIN_ID, isSkinId } from \"@/lib/game/playerSkins\";\nimport { MAX_PROFILE_NAME } from \"@/lib/game/profiles\";\nimport { authClient, type OnlineUser } from \"@/lib/auth/client\";\nimport { createOnlineProfile, deleteOnlineProfile, listOnlineProfiles, updateOnlineProfile, type OnlineProfile } from \"@/lib/online/profilesClient\";\nimport { skinPortraitUrl } from \"@/lib/ui/sprites\";\n\n/**\n * The signed-in account's home: its server-side profiles (name + skin), synced\n * across devices. The account-mode counterpart to ProfileSelect — picking a\n * profile shows that profile's online worlds. Local Players (logged out) never\n * see this; guests can't have profiles. Capped at MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES.\n */\n\ntype AccountProfileSelectProps = {\n  user: OnlineUser;\n  /** Enter an online profile: show its server-hosted worlds. */\n  onPlay: (profile: OnlineProfile) => void;\n  /** After signing out — the caller drops back to the local (logged-out) menu. */\n  onSignedOut: () => void;\n};\n\nexport default function AccountProfileSelect({ user, onPlay, onSignedOut }: AccountProfileSelectProps) {\n  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<OnlineProfile[] | null>(null);\n  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);\n  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [editName, setEditName] = useState(\"\");\n  const [confirmingDeleteId, setConfirmingDeleteId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n\n  const refresh = () => void listOnlineProfiles().then(setProfiles);\n  useEffect(() => refresh(), []);\n\n  const signOut = async () => {\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      await authClient().signOut();\n      onSignedOut();\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const atCap = (profiles?.length ?? 0) >= MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES;\n\n  if (creating) {\n    return (\n      <MenuScreen title=\"New Profile\">\n        <CreateProfileForm\n          onCreate={(name, skinId) => {\n            void createOnlineProfile({ name, skinId }).then((profile) => {\n              setCreating(false);\n              if (profile) onPlay(profile);\n              else refresh(); // create failed (e.g. hit the cap) — re-sync the list\n            });\n          }}\n          onCancel={() => setCreating(false)}\n        />\n      </MenuScreen>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <MenuScreen title=\"Your Profiles\">\n      <div className=\"account-panel\">\n        <span className=\"account-status\">Signed in as {user.name}</span>\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"mc-button\" onClick={signOut} disabled={busy}>\n          Sign out\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      {profiles === null ? (\n        <p className=\"menu-empty\">Loading…</p>\n      ) : profiles.length === 0 ? (\n        <p className=\"menu-empty\">No profiles yet — create one to play online.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <ul className=\"menu-list\">\n          {profiles.map((profile) => (\n            <li key={profile.id} className=\"menu-card\">\n              {editingId === profile.id ? (\n                <form\n                  className=\"menu-rename\"\n                  onSubmit={(event) => {\n                    event.preventDefault();\n                    void updateOnlineProfile(profile.id, { name: editName }).then(() => {\n                      setEditingId(null);\n                      refresh();\n                    });\n                  }}\n                >\n                  <input\n                    className=\"menu-input\"\n                    value={editName}\n                    maxLength={MAX_PROFILE_NAME}\n                    autoFocus\n                    aria-label=\"Rename profile\"\n                    onChange={(event) => setEditName(event.target.value)}\n                  />\n                  <button type=\"submit\" className=\"mc-button\">\n                    Save\n                  </button>\n                  <button type=\"button\" className=\"mc-button\" onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}>\n                    Cancel\n                  </button>\n                </form>\n              ) : confirmingDeleteId === profile.id ? (\n                <div className=\"menu-confirm\">\n                  <span>Delete “{profile.name}” and all its online worlds?</span>\n                  <div className=\"menu-confirm-actions\">\n                    <button\n                      className=\"mc-button danger\"\n                      onClick={() =>\n                        void deleteOnlineProfile(profile.id).then(() => {\n                          setConfirmingDeleteId(null);\n                          refresh();\n                        })\n                      }\n                    >\n                      Delete\n                    </button>\n                    <button className=\"mc-button\" onClick={() => setConfirmingDeleteId(null)}>\n                      Cancel\n                    </button>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              ) : (\n                <>\n                  <button className=\"menu-card-play\" data-testid={`online-profile-${profile.id}`} onClick={() => onPlay(profile)}>\n                    <PixelImg src={skinPortraitUrl(isSkinId(profile.skinId) ? profile.skinId : DEFAULT_SKIN_ID)} alt=\"\" size={40} aria-hidden />\n                    <span className=\"menu-card-name\">{profile.name}</span>\n                  </button>\n                  <div className=\"menu-card-actions\">\n                    <button\n                      className=\"mc-button\"\n                      onClick={() => {\n                        setEditName(profile.name);\n                        setEditingId(profile.id);\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      Rename\n                    </button>\n                    <button className=\"mc-button\" onClick={() => setConfirmingDeleteId(profile.id)}>\n                      Delete\n                    </button>\n                  </div>\n                </>\n              )}\n            </li>\n          ))}\n        </ul>\n      )}\n      <button\n        className=\"mc-button menu-primary\"\n        data-testid=\"new-online-profile\"\n        disabled={atCap}\n        title={atCap ? `Profile limit reached (${MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES})` : undefined}\n        onClick={() => setCreating(true)}\n      >\n        {atCap ? `Profile limit reached (${MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES})` : \"New Profile\"}\n      </button>\n    </MenuScreen>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "type Screen =\n  | { name: \"profile-select\" }\n  | { name: \"world-select\"; profileId: string }\n  | { name: \"play\"; profileId: string; worldId: string }\n  | { name: \"play-online\"; profileId: string; world: OnlineWorld; session: NetworkSession };\n\n/** Online worlds mount the same game subtree; the meta is a projection of the server row. */\nfunction onlineWorldMeta(world: OnlineWorld, profileId: string): WorldMeta {",
  "new_string": "type Screen =\n  | { name: \"profile-select\" }\n  | { name: \"world-select\"; profileId: string }\n  | { name: \"online-worlds\"; profile: OnlineProfile }\n  | { name: \"play\"; profileId: string; worldId: string }\n  // play-online carries the resolved player identity (a local Profile for a\n  // guest, or one derived from the account profile) plus that account profile\n  // (null for the guest path) so \"quit to worlds\" returns to the right list.\n  | { name: \"play-online\"; profile: Profile; world: OnlineWorld; session: NetworkSession; onlineProfile: OnlineProfile | null };\n\n/** A play-usable Profile from a server-side account profile (skin sanitized). */\nfunction profileFromOnline(profile: OnlineProfile): Profile {\n  return { id: profile.id, name: profile.name, skinId: isSkinId(profile.skinId) ? profile.skinId : DEFAULT_SKIN_ID, createdAt: 0 };\n}\n\n/** Online worlds mount the same game subtree; the meta is a projection of the server row. */\nfunction onlineWorldMeta(world: OnlineWorld, profileId: string): WorldMeta {"
}

  "new_string": "  // A join is in flight — guards against a double-click opening (and leaking) a\n  // second socket. A ref, not `connecting`, so it's set synchronously.\n  const joiningRef = useRef(false);\n  // The signed-in account (a real, non-anonymous one flips the menu into\n  // account mode). Offline-first: never asked until this browser went online.\n  const [onlineUser, setOnlineUser] = useState<OnlineUser | null>(null);\n  const refreshOnlineUser = useCallback(() => {\n    if (onlineUsed()) void currentUser().then(setOnlineUser);\n  }, []);"
}

Now refactor `playOnline` to take a resolved `Profile` + the account profile, and thread the ticket profileId:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01VjwWuzGKsz62U3p35U2hNW
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/components/GameShell.tsx",
  "old_string": "  /** Guest-or-account → ticket → socket → replica sync → play. */\n  const playOnline = async (profileId: string, world: OnlineWorld) => {\n    if (joiningRef.current) return; // a join is already in flight\n    joiningRef.current = true;\n    setConnectError(null);\n    setConnecting(world.name);\n    try {\n      if (!(await ensureSignedIn())) throw new Error(\"sign-in failed\");\n      const grant = await requestJoinTicket(world.id);\n      if (!grant) throw new Error(\"could not get a join ticket (is the game server configured?)\");\n      // The reconnect ladder mints a fresh short-lived ticket each retry — the\n      // 60 s TTL means a stale one is useless a minute after a drop.\n      const session = await connectNetworkSession(grant.gameServerUrl, grant.ticket, undefined, {\n        reconnect: async () => {\n          const next = await requestJoinTicket(world.id);\n          return next ? { url: next.gameServerUrl, ticket: next.ticket } : null;\n        }\n      });\n      setScreen({ name: \"play-online\", profileId, world, session });\n    } catch (error) {\n      setConnectError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"connection failed\");\n    } finally {\n      setConnecting(null);\n      joiningRef.current = false;\n    }\n  };",
  "new_string": "  /** Guest-or-account → ticket → socket → replica sync → play. When an account\n   *  profile is given, its id rides the ticket so the roster shows that profile. */\n  const playOnline = async (profile: Profile, world: OnlineWorld, onlineProfile: OnlineProfile | null) => {\n    if (joiningRef.current) return; // a join is already in flight\n    joiningRef.current = true;\n    setConnectError(null);\n    setConnecting(world.name);\n    const ticketProfileId = onlineProfile?.id;\n    try {\n      if (!(await ensureSignedIn())) throw new Error(\"sign-in failed\");\n      const grant = await requestJoinTicket(world.id, ticketProfileId);\n      if (!grant) throw new Error(\"could not get a join ticket (is the game server configured?)\");\n      // The reconnect ladder mints a fresh short-lived ticket each retry — the\n      // 60 s TTL means a stale one is useless a minute after a drop.\n      const session = await connectNetworkSession(grant.gameServerUrl, grant.ticket, undefined, {\n        reconnect: async () => {\n          const next = await requestJoinTicket(world.id, ticketProfileId);\n          return next ? { url: next.gameServerUrl, ticket: next.ticket } : null;\n        }\n      });\n      setScreen({ name: \"play-online\", profile, world, session, onlineProfile });\n    } catch (error) {\n      setConnectError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"connection failed\");\n    } finally {\n      setConnecting(null);\n      joiningRef.current = false;\n    }\n  };"
}
```

  "old_string": "  if (screen.name === \"play-online\") {\n    const profile = getProfile(screen.profileId);\n    if (profile) {\n      return (\n        <MinecraftGame\n          key={`online:${screen.world.id}`}\n          world={onlineWorldMeta(screen.world, profile.id)}\n          profile={profile}\n          online={screen.session}\n          onQuitToWorlds={() => setScreen({ name: \"world-select\", profileId: profile.id })}\n          onDeleteWorld={() => {\n            // Hardcore game-over: actually delete the shared world on the server\n            // (owner-gated; a member just leaves), then return to the list.\n            screen.session.dispose();\n            void deleteOnlineWorld(screen.world.id);\n            setScreen({ name: \"world-select\", profileId: profile.id });\n          }}\n          onReloadWorld={() => setScreen({ name: \"world-select\", profileId: profile.id })}\n        />\n      );\n    }\n  }",
  "new_string": "  if (screen.name === \"play-online\") {\n    // Quit returns to the account profile's online worlds, or (guest path) the\n    // local world list the join came from.\n    const backToWorlds: Screen = screen.onlineProfile ? { name: \"online-worlds\", profile: screen.onlineProfile } : { name: \"world-select\", profileId: screen.profile.id };\n    return (\n      <MinecraftGame\n        key={`online:${screen.world.id}`}\n        world={onlineWorldMeta(screen.world, screen.profile.id)}\n        profile={screen.profile}\n        online={screen.session}\n        onQuitToWorlds={() => setScreen(backToWorlds)}\n        onDeleteWorld={() => {\n          // Hardcore game-over: actually delete the shared world on the server\n          // (owner-gated; a member just leaves), then return to the list.\n          screen.session.dispose();\n          void deleteOnlineWorld(screen.world.id);\n          setScreen(backToWorlds);\n        }}\n        onReloadWorld={() => setScreen(backToWorlds)}\n      />\n    );\n  }"
}

The\nconnection modals are hoisted into a shared netModals.\n"
}

profileId?: string }) => {\n    const created = mpWorld(`new-${fake.worlds.length}`, profileId ?? \"\", { name });\n    fake.worlds = [...fake.worlds, created];\n    return created;\n  },\n  createInviteLink: async () => \"http://localhost/join/tok\",\n  revokeInviteLinks: async () => 1\n}));\n\nconst { default: OnlineWorldSelect } = await import(\"./OnlineWorldSelect\");\n\nconst profile = { id: \"p1\", name: \"Steve\", skinId: \"default\", createdAt: \"1\" };\n\ndescribe(\"OnlineWorldSelect\", () => {\n  test(\"lists only this profile's mp worlds and joins the chosen one\", async () => {\n    fake.worlds = [mpWorld(\"w1\", \"p1\"), mpWorld(\"w2\", \"p2\"), mpWorld(\"w3\", \"p1\", { kind: \"sp-cloud\" })];\n    const onPlay = mock();\n    render(<OnlineWorldSelect profile={profile} onPlay={onPlay} onBack={mock()} />);\n    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(\"W-w1\")).toBeTruthy());\n    expect(screen.queryByText(\"W-w2\")).toBeNull(); // a different profile's world\n    expect(screen.queryByText(\"W-w3\")).toBeNull(); // sp-cloud, not an online room\n\n    await userEvent.click(screen.getByTestId(\"online-world-w1\"));\n    expect(onPlay).toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  test(\"creating an online world enters it\", async () => {\n    fake.worlds = [];\n    const onPlay = mock();\n    render(<OnlineWorldSelect profile={profile} onPlay={onPlay} onBack={mock()} />);\n    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(/No online worlds yet/)).toBeTruthy());\n\n    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"New Online World\" }));\n    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(\"World name\"), \"Co-op\");\n    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"Create World\" }));\n    await waitFor(() => expect(onPlay).toHaveBeenCalled());\n  });\n\n  test(\"New Online World is disabled at the per-profile world cap\", async () => {\n    fake.worlds = Array.from({ length: MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE }, (_, i) => mpWorld(`w${i}`, \"p1\"));\n    render(<OnlineWorldSelect profile={profile} onPlay={mock()} onBack={mock()} />);\n    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText(\"W-w0\")).toBeTruthy());\n    expect((screen.getByTestId(\"new-online-world\") as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/CHANGELOG.md",
  "old_string": "- **Account profiles (groundwork)**: the data layer for the \"Local Players vs Accounts\" model — a signed-in account can own up to `MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES` (**5**) server-side profiles (cross-device identities: name + skin), each owning up to `MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE` (**10**) online worlds. New `profiles` table + a `profileId` on `worlds` (drizzle migration `0001`, with a backfill that gives every existing non-guest account a default profile and hands it their worlds); owner-scoped profile CRUD with server-enforced quotas in `worldsService.ts`; `/api/profiles` routes + a browser client (`lib/online/profilesClient.ts`); and join tickets now carry the **chosen profile's** name/skin, so the in-world roster shows the profile rather than the account. Guests (anonymous) can't create profiles — online identities are account-only. Additive and offline-first: single-player and the existing online flow are unchanged until the menu is wired to profiles.",
  "new_string": "- **Account profiles & account-mode menu**: the \"Local Players vs Accounts\" model — a signed-in account owns up to `MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES` (**5**) server-side profiles (cross-device identities: name + skin), each owning up to `MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE` (**10**) online worlds. New `profiles` table + a `profileId` on `worlds` (drizzle migration `0001`, with a backfill that gives every existing non-guest account a default profile and hands it their worlds); owner-scoped profile CRUD with server-enforced quotas in `worldsService.ts`; `/api/profiles` routes + a browser client (`lib/online/profilesClient.ts`); and join tickets now carry the **chosen profile's** name/skin, so the in-world roster shows the profile rather than the account. Guests (anonymous) can't create profiles — online identities are account-only. When signed in, the menu opens into an **account home** (`AccountProfileSelect`) listing your profiles (create/rename/delete), and inside each, its online worlds (`OnlineWorldSelect` — create/invite/join). Logged-out **Local Players** keep the browser-local profile flow unchanged, and single-player never touches the network. (Guests still reach online play via the legacy path until the anonymous layer is retired.)"
}
