5:- **`bun test`** — unit, integration, and component tests, colocated as `*.test.ts[x]` next to the code they cover. The shared setup (`tests/setup.ts`, preloaded via `bunfig.toml`) registers happy-dom so React component tests run under the same runner; the game engine itself needs no DOM (see [architecture.md](architecture.md)).
17:| Online accounts & worlds      | `lib/auth/auth.test.ts`, `lib/online/worldsService.test.ts`, `lib/net/tickets.test.ts`, `lib/game/cloudSaves.test.ts`, `components/menu/AccountPanel.test.tsx`                                                                                                | The guest→account upgrade re-parents worlds/memberships/invites (real better-auth on PGlite, incl. the duplicate-membership collision); membership/owner gating, idempotent invites with expiry/max-uses, LWW save-blob stale guard, mp-only verifiable join tickets (sign/tamper/expiry/garbage), gzip codec round-trip, the Account panel's offline→guest→upgrade flow, and account profiles (owner-scoped CRUD, the `MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES`/`MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE` quotas, guests barred from creating profiles, profile-delete cascading its worlds, and a join ticket carrying the chosen profile's name+skin)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
24:| XP & enchanting               | `lib/game/engine/systems/xp.test.ts`, `lib/game/enchantments.test.ts`, `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts`, `components/game/{XpBar,EnchantingColumn}.test.tsx`                                                                                              | XP level/progress math, award accumulation, spend afford/refuse, per-mob/per-ore tables; XP on a kill (not a baby), the eleven enchant seams (Sharpness +damage, Power/Punch on a fired arrow, Knockback +melee shove, Looting +mob drops, Protection +armor reduction, Feather Falling −fall damage, Efficiency ×mining speed, Fortune +ore drops, Unbreaking skips wear seeded, Mending repair), `canEnchant`/`applyEnchant` gating + immutability + the `itemIds` allow-list (Power/Punch bow-only, Feather Falling boots-only); the enchant command (applies + spends levels + emits; refused off-table / broke / wrong kind), right-click table opens the station; XP bar fill + hidden level 0, the EnchantingColumn afford/locked states                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
26:| Advancements & stats          | `lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.test.ts`, `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts`, `lib/game/save.test.ts`, `components/game/AdvancementsPanel.test.tsx`                                                                                                   | `recordEvent` maps each event to the right counter (per-ore, hostile-only kills via `HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS`, the new `crafted` event → items_crafted / aggregate pickaxes / villager_trades) and `recordTick` accumulates play time + distance; `evaluateAdvancements` unlocks exactly at threshold, never twice, in registry order, and every advancement is reachable; registry integrity (unique ids, icon/category/threshold present). Through the engine: crafting a pickaxe / killing a hostile emit `advancementUnlocked` **once** via the single emit chokepoint, the snapshot unlocked-count + `advancementState()` expose progression to the UI, the L-key overlay toggles mutually-exclusive with the inventory, and **both stats and advancements survive a save round-trip and a death** (not cleared, unlike effects). Save side: `migrateSaveV12toV13` pure bump + `serializeStats`/`restoreStats`/`restoreAdvancements` round-trip and drop garbage. Component: `AdvancementsPanel` renders locked vs unlocked entries, switches tabs, and formats counts / distance / duration                                                                                                                                     |
35:| Villages, professions & raids | `lib/game/engine/systems/{spawnDirector,raid}.test.ts`, `lib/game/{trades,save}.test.ts`, `components/game/InventoryPanel.test.tsx`, `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts`, `lib/world/generation.test.ts`                                                     | `spawnVillageResidents` seeds villagers per site and `assignVillagerProfessions` cycles professions round-robin (leaving restored ones alone); every trade is tagged with a valid profession and each profession has a two-sided economy; opening a villager filters the Trading book to its offers and the craft command refuses another profession's trade; v15 migration + a resident's `profession` round-trips (invalid ones dropped); a fresh world persists its residents but no pets. Raids: `startRaid` refuses a second/Peaceful raid; `tickRaid` spawns waves, wins (emerald + XP) when all are cleared, loses when the nearby villagers die, and Peaceful cancels it; sounding an Ominous Horn near a village starts a raid and consumes the horn (refused while one runs)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

**Phase 2 UI (the auth-aware menu) is done — 3 commits, all local, nothing pushed.**

```
1dd8961 docs: account-mode menu (online profiles & worlds)
2beeb71 test(menu): cover AccountProfileSelect and OnlineWorldSelect

```

**What it does:** when you're signed in as a real account, the menu now opens into an **account home** (`AccountProfileSelect`) listing your server-side profiles — create (capped at 5), rename, delete, sign out — and picking one opens `OnlineWorldSelect`, that profile's online worlds (create capped at 10, copy/revoke invites, join). Join tickets carry the chosen **profile's** name/skin. Logged-out **Local Players** and guests keep the existing browser-local flow unchanged (removing guest-online is Phase 3), so this is fully additive.

**Verified locally** (format + lint per commit, plus a one-time feature check): typecheck, lint, format, the new component tests (7 pass), and **menu + multiplayer e2e (6 pass)** — confirming the GameShell refactor didn't break local single-player or the guest online flow.

**Two caveats worth flagging:**
- There's **no full-stack e2e yet for the account online journey** (sign up → create profile → create online world → join). The component tests cover the menu logic, but the real account-play path isn't exercised end-to-end. That naturally pairs with Phase 3 (rewriting the guest-based `multiplayer.e2e.ts` to use a real account).
- Guest-online still works via the legacy path — intentional; **Phase 3** retires it (remove the anonymous layer, drop "Play online as guest", require sign-in on invites, migrate, and rewrite `docs/online.md`).

Nothing is pushed. Where next — do you want to review/push these first, or should I go on to **Phase 3**?

how may phases do we left? only phase 3?

> AGENT

Yes — **only Phase 3 is left.** The plan was three phases and 1 and 2 are both landed on #46.

Phase 3 is the largest and riskiest, though — it's the "flip to accounts-only" cleanup:

- **Remove the anonymous-guest layer** — the `anonymous()` plugin + `onLinkAccount`, `ensureSignedIn`, the `isAnonymous` column, and the guest branches in `AccountPanel`.
- **Drop "Play online as guest"** from the menu; logged-out = Local Player only.
- **Invite links require sign-in** — `app/join/[token]` routes an unauthenticated visitor to sign-in/register, then pick a profile.
- **Rewrite `multiplayer.e2e.ts`** to use a real account (this also fills the missing account-play e2e gap).
- **Migrate existing deployed data** — drop/handle guest-owned online worlds.
- **Rewrite `docs/online.md`** ("Identity: guests first" → accounts-for-online).

Two decisions to settle inside it (from the plan's open list): whether existing guest-owned online worlds get **dropped with a release note** (my default) or a one-time upgrade prompt, and whether to leave save-slice keying at the account level (current) or move it to per-profile.

So: one phase, but a meaty one. Want me to plan it out and get those two decisions from you before starting?

> AGENT

Here's the full status on **PR #46**.

## CI

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| **verify** | ❌ **failed (27s)** — at `typecheck` |
| e2e | ⏳ pending |
| Vercel / CodeRabbit / Preview | ✅ pass |

**The verify failure is my bug and trivial:** `components/menu/AccountProfileSelect.test.tsx:64` uses `getByRole("button", { name: "Create", exact: true })` — `exact` is a Playwright option, not valid in Testing Library's types. It slipped through because after the test slice I ran only format+lint (per your preference) and the runtime test ignored the stray prop; CI's typecheck caught it. Fix = delete `, exact: true` (that view has only one "Create" button, so it's unambiguous).

## CodeRabbit — 8 findings, triaged

**Worth fixing (quick wins):**
- **`worldsService.ts:58` (Major)** — I redeclared `MAX_PROFILE_NAME` instead of reusing the one `lib/game/profiles.ts` already exports. Reuse it.
- **`AccountProfileSelect.tsx:48` (Major)** — my `signOut` has no `catch`/error feedback (unlike the `AccountPanel` one we just hardened). Mirror that fix.
- **`AccountProfileSelect.tsx:67` (Minor)** — a failed profile-create is swallowed (form closes, no message). Keep the form open with an error.
- **`db/schema.ts:94` (Minor, perf)** — no index on `profiles.owner_id` / `worlds.profile_id`, which every list/quota query filters on. Cheap to add (needs a small follow-up migration).

**Judgment calls:**
- **`worldsService.ts:176` (Critical) + `:90`** — TOCTOU race: the profile/world **quota** is `count-then-insert` with no transaction, so two concurrent creates from the *same account* could slip past the cap by one. It's real but low-impact — a **soft cap**, same-user only, and the UI creates one at a time; worst case is one extra row. A truly race-free fix means a transaction + advisory lock (or a DB constraint), which is heavier and needs PGlite-compat checking.
- **`GameShell.tsx:281` (Major) + `AccountProfileSelect.tsx:131` (Minor)** — fire-and-forget `deleteOnlineWorld` / ignored rename-delete results. The GameShell one is **pre-existing** (I only moved it); the rename/delete case self-corrects on the next `refresh()`. Low value.

**My recommendation:** apply the four quick wins + the typecheck fix now; for the Critical TOCTOU, I'd add the advisory-lock-in-transaction (it's the honest fix for a "Critical" flag) unless you'd rather accept it as a documented soft-cap limitation. The two fire-and-forget ones I'd skip.

How do you want to handle the TOCTOU — **proper lock** or **accept as soft cap** — and shall I apply the quick wins + typecheck fix (committed locally, not pushed until you say)?

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/online/worldsService.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function createProfile(\n  db: Db,\n  owner: { id: string; isAnonymous: boolean },\n  input: { name: string; skinId?: string | null }\n): Promise<{ ok: true; profile: ProfileSummary } | Failure> {\n  if (owner.isAnonymous) return fail(\"forbidden\");\n  const name = input.name?.trim();\n  if (!name || name.length > MAX_PROFILE_NAME) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  const existing = await db.select({ id: schema.profiles.id }).from(schema.profiles).where(eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, owner.id));\n  if (existing.length >= MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES) return fail(\"conflict\");\n  const [profile] = await db\n    .insert(schema.profiles)\n    .values({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), ownerId: owner.id, name, skinId: input.skinId ?? null })\n    .returning();\n  return { ok: true, profile: toProfileSummary(profile) };\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function createProfile(\n  db: Db,\n  owner: { id: string; isAnonymous: boolean },\n  input: { name: string; skinId?: string | null }\n): Promise<{ ok: true; profile: ProfileSummary } | Failure> {\n  if (owner.isAnonymous) return fail(\"forbidden\");\n  const name = input.name?.trim();\n  if (!name || name.length > MAX_PROFILE_NAME) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  // Count and insert under a per-account lock so two concurrent creates can't\n  // both slip past MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES.\n  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    await tx.execute(lockAccount(owner.id));\n    const existing = await tx.select({ id: schema.profiles.id }).from(schema.profiles).where(eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, owner.id));\n    if (existing.length >= MAX_ONLINE_PROFILES) return fail(\"conflict\");\n    const [profile] = await tx\n      .insert(schema.profiles)\n      .values({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), ownerId: owner.id, name, skinId: input.skinId ?? null })\n      .returning();\n    return { ok: true, profile: toProfileSummary(profile) };\n  });\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/online/worldsService.ts",
  "old_string": "  const name = input.name?.trim();\n  if (!name || name.length > 64) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  if (input.kind !== \"sp-cloud\" && input.kind !== \"mp\") return fail(\"invalid\");\n  if (!Number.isFinite(input.seed)) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  let profileId: string | null = null;\n  if (input.profileId) {\n    // The profile must be the caller's, and it can't already be at its world cap.\n    const [profile] = await db\n      .select({ id: schema.profiles.id })\n      .from(schema.profiles)\n      .where(and(eq(schema.profiles.id, input.profileId), eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, userId)));\n    if (!profile) return fail(\"forbidden\");\n    const owned = await db.select({ id: schema.worlds.id }).from(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.profileId, input.profileId));\n    if (owned.length >= MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE) return fail(\"conflict\");\n    profileId = input.profileId;\n  }\n  const id = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const [world] = await db\n    .insert(schema.worlds)\n    .values({\n      id,\n      ownerId: userId,\n      profileId,\n      kind: input.kind,\n      name,\n      seed: Math.floor(input.seed),\n      worldType: input.worldType ?? \"default\",\n      gameMode: input.gameMode ?? \"survival\",\n      difficulty: input.difficulty ?? \"normal\",\n      hardcore: input.hardcore ?? false,\n      worldgenVersion: WORLDGEN_VERSION\n    })\n    .returning();\n  await db.insert(schema.worldMembers).values({ worldId: id, userId, role: \"owner\" });\n  return { ok: true, world: toSummary(world, \"owner\") };\n}",
  "new_string": "  const name = input.name?.trim();\n  if (!name || name.length > 64) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  if (input.kind !== \"sp-cloud\" && input.kind !== \"mp\") return fail(\"invalid\");\n  if (!Number.isFinite(input.seed)) return fail(\"invalid\");\n  // One transaction so the world + owner-membership inserts are atomic; when a\n  // profile is named, a per-account lock also serializes the world-cap check.\n  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    let profileId: string | null = null;\n    if (input.profileId) {\n      await tx.execute(lockAccount(userId));\n      const [profile] = await tx\n        .select({ id: schema.profiles.id })\n        .from(schema.profiles)\n        .where(and(eq(schema.profiles.id, input.profileId), eq(schema.profiles.ownerId, userId)));\n      if (!profile) return fail(\"forbidden\"); // not the caller's profile\n      const owned = await tx.select({ id: schema.worlds.id }).from(schema.worlds).where(eq(schema.worlds.profileId, input.profileId));\n      if (owned.length >= MAX_WORLDS_PER_PROFILE) return fail(\"conflict\");\n      profileId = input.profileId;\n    }\n    const id = crypto.randomUUID();\n    const [world] = await tx\n      .insert(schema.worlds)\n      .values({\n        id,\n        ownerId: userId,\n        profileId,\n        kind: input.kind,\n        name,\n        seed: Math.floor(input.seed),\n        worldType: input.worldType ?? \"default\",\n        gameMode: input.gameMode ?? \"survival\",\n        difficulty: input.difficulty ?? \"normal\",\n        hardcore: input.hardcore ?? false,\n        worldgenVersion: WORLDGEN_VERSION\n      })\n      .returning();\n    await tx.insert(schema.worldMembers).values({ worldId: id, userId, role: \"owner\" });\n    return { ok: true, world: toSummary(world, \"owner\") };\n  });\n}"
}

Done — the CodeRabbit fixes are in as **3 local commits (not pushed)**:

```
a89a01e perf(db): index profiles.owner_id and worlds.profile_id
a18ce81 fix(online): reuse MAX_PROFILE_NAME and close the quota TOCTOU race
0ab63b5 fix(menu): surface AccountProfileSelect sign-out/create failures
```

**What each addresses:**
- **CI typecheck (the red `verify`)** — removed the invalid `getByRole({ exact: true })`. Typecheck is now green locally.
- **TOCTOU (Critical) + `:90`** — profile and world caps now count-and-insert inside a transaction guarded by a per-account **advisory lock** (`pg_advisory_xact_lock`), so concurrent creates from one account can't race past a cap. Verified PGlite supports it (16 service tests pass). `createWorld`'s world + membership inserts are atomic now too.
- **Duplicate `MAX_PROFILE_NAME` (Major)** — reused the one `lib/game/profiles.ts` exports.
- **`AccountProfileSelect` sign-out/create feedback (Major/Minor)** — sign-out now has a `catch` + visible error; a failed create keeps the form open with a message. Added a sign-out-failure test.
- **Missing indexes (Minor, perf)** — btree indexes on `profiles.owner_id` and `worlds.profile_id` (migration `0002`).

**Skipped, with reason** (2 findings): the fire-and-forget `deleteOnlineWorld` in `GameShell` (Major) is **pre-existing** — I only moved it — and it's a best-effort game-over delete the server re-reconciles; the ignored rename/delete result (Minor) **self-corrects** on the next `refresh()`. Happy to address either if you'd prefer.

Verified locally: typecheck, lint, format, and the affected tests (33 pass). Nothing pushed. **Push to #46** so CI re-runs green, or hold?

I think that's the right call — here's my honest take, plus one clarification that makes it easier.

**Yes, do Phase 3 in a fresh session.** Reasons:
- This session is very long (Phase 1 + Phase 2 backend + Phase 2 UI + two rounds of review fixes). Phase 3 is the **biggest and riskiest** phase — it deletes tested subsystems, migrates live prod data, and rewrites the invite flow + e2e. A clean context materially lowers the chance of a mistake based on stale/compacted context.
- Continuity is already preserved: `identity-rework.md` in memory and the plan file both capture exactly where Phase 3 resumes (the TODO list, file pointers, and the two open decisions). A new session picks up cleanly.
- It's a natural checkpoint — let #46's CI/CodeRabbit settle and give yourself a chance to review it properly before layering the destructive phase on.

**The clarification (good news):** because I built Phase 2 **additively** — guests are completely untouched, still working via the legacy path — **#46 is safe to merge on its own.** My earlier "Phases 2+3 must ship together" caution no longer applies: I never landed the anonymous-*gating* in Phase 2, so there's no half-migrated prod state. So you can merge #46 whenever CI's green, and **Phase 3 becomes its own clean follow-up PR** in the new session.
